uriyāciriyar

patippāciriyarkaḷ
muṉivar oḷavi naṭarācaṉ

iṉiyamutu patippakam
ceṉṉi - 600 004.

nūṟ kuṟippu
nūṟpeyar	:	urivēntar 							tamiḻttoki - 16
uriyāciriyar 	:	oḷavi turicāmi
patippāḷar	:	i. tamiḻamutu
patippu	:	2009
tāḷ	:	16 ki veḷḷittāḷ
aḷavu	:	1/8 temmi
eḻuttu	:	11 puḷḷi
pakkam	:	8 + 440 = 448
nūl kaṭṭamippu	:	iyalpu (cātāraṇam)
vili	:	urupā. 280/-
paṭikaḷ	:	1000
nūlākkam	:	pāvāṇar kaṇiṉi
		ti.nakar, ceṉṉi - 17.

veḷiyīṭu
iṉiyamutu patippakam
pi.11 kulmokar kuṭiyiruppu, 
35 cevāliya civāci kaṇēcaṉ cāli, 
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017. 
to.pē. 2433 9030

patippuri
oḷavi cu.turicāmip piḷḷi
tamatu ōyvaṟiyā uḻippāl  tamiḻ āyvuk kaḷattil uyarntu niṉṟavar. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku puttuyir ūṭṭiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷuḷ muṉ variciyil niṟpavar. naṟṟiṇi, puṟanāṉūṟu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu ākiya caṅka nūṟ celvaṅkaḷukku urivaḷam kaṇṭavar. civa peruṅkaṭalil mūḻkit tiḷittavar. urivēntar eṉṟu tamiḻulakam pōṟṟip pukaḻappaṭṭa oḷavi cu.turicāmip piḷḷi 1903il piṟantu 1981il maṟintār. 
 vāḻnta āṇṭukaḷ 78. eḻutiya nūlkaḷ 38. itaṉi  poruḷ vaḻip pirittu “urivēntar tamiḻttoki” eṉum talippil 28 tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm.
illaṟa ēntalākavum, urinayam kaṇṭa uravōrākavum , naṟṟamiḻ nāvalarākavum, civa cittāntac cemmalākavum , niṟipukaḻ eytiya uri vēntarākavum, pulamiyilum perum pulamipeṟṟavarākavum tikaḻntu viḷaṅkiya ipperuntamiḻācāṉiṉ nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum oru cēra veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟōm. ivaruṭiya nūlkaḷil  em kikkuk kiṭikkap peṟāta nūlkaḷ 5. maṟṟum ivar eḻutiya tiruvaruṭpā  nūlkaḷum it tokutikaḷil iṭam peṟavilli. 
  “ palvēṟu kālat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, urikaḷ, varalāṟu, kalveṭṭu, camayaṅkaḷ eṉṟiṉṉa tuṟip palavaṟṟil niṟipulami peṟṟavar oḷavi cu .turicāmi avarkaḷ” eṉṟu mūtaṟiñar va.cupa. māṇikkam avarkaḷālum,
  “iravupakal tāṉaṟiyāṉ iṉtamiḻi vittu
	 varavu celavaṟiyāṉ vāḻvil - uramuṭiyāṉ
	 taṉkaṭaṉ tāynāṭṭu makkaṭ kuḻippatilē
	 muṉkaṭaṉ eṉṟurikkum ēṟu” 
eṉṟu pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avarkaḷālum pōṟṟip pukaḻap paṭṭa ipperuntakiyiṉ nūlkaḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷākak kōrttu, muttumāliyākak  koṭuttuḷḷōm.   
 avar kālattil vāḻnta camakāla aṟiñarkaḷāl pōṟṟip pukaḻap paṭṭavar.  civa ulakil taṉakkeṉat taṉiyiṭattip peṟṟavar.  ivar eḻutiya aṉittu nūlkaḷ maṟṟum malarkaḷ, itaḻkaḷil veḷivanta kaṭṭurikaḷiyellām tēṭit tēṭi eṭuttu orē vīccil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. 
ittokutikaḷ aṉittum mikacciṟappāka veḷivaruvataṟku muḻuottuḻippum utaviyum nalkiyavarkaḷ avaruṭiya tirumakaṉ oḷavi tu.naṭarācaṉ, marukar irā.kumaravēlaṉ, makaḷ vayiṟṟup peyartti tirumati vēṉilā sṭāliṉ ākiyōr āvar. ivarkaḷ it tamiḻttokikku takka matippuriyum aḷittu eṅkaḷukkup perumic cērttu uḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷukku neñcam niṟinta naṉṟi
taṉ matippu iyakkattil pērīṭupāṭu koṇṭu  uḻitta ivvaruntamiḻaṟiñar tamiḻp pakivarit tam pakivarākak koṇṭa uyar maṉattiṉarāka vāḻntavar eṉpati niṉivil koṇṭu it toki  nūlkaḷi  ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñariṉ 
107  ām āṇṭu niṉivāka ulakat tamiḻarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil  veḷiyiṭuvatil perumakiḻvu koḷkiṟōm. tamiḻ nūl patippil eṅkaḷiṉ inta ariya muyaṟcikkut tōḷ tantu utavuṅkaḷ.
naṉṟi
- patippāḷar

muṉṉuri
caṅkattoki nūlkaḷuḷ eṭṭut tokiyil mutaṟkaṇ eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭum ēṟṟam uṭiyatu naṟṟiṇi. mutal iru nūṟu pāṭṭukkaḷ aṭaṅkiya mutaṟṟokuti ceṉṟa 1966-il veḷiyāyiṟṟu. 201-400 aṭaṅkiya iraṇṭām tokuti ippoḻutu veḷivarukiṟa teṉpati mikka makiḻcciyuṭaṉ terivikkiṉṟēṉ. mēlum, innaṟṟiṇi muṟṟum viḷakkavuriyuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭum vakiyil eṉatu tamiḻp pulami vāḻkkiyil yāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kuṟikkōḷ iṉitu niṟivēṟukiṟa teṉṟu mikka makiḻcci  koḷkiṉṟēṉ.
cumār nāṟpatu āṇṭukaṭku muṉ yāṉ tamiḻaṟivu ōraḷavu peṟṟup paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷip payiṉṟu iṉpuṟṟa kāli, cila nūlkaḷ kuṟiyuṟṟiruntami kaṇṭu, eṅṅaṉamēṉum muyaṉṟu niṟivu ceyvatu tamiḻaṉṉikkuc ceyyat takka paṇi yeṉṟa karutti uṭkoṇṭatōṭu, ataṉiyē eṉ vāḻviṉ kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭēṉ. annāḷil karantik kaviyaracu. ār.vēṅkaṭācalampiḷḷiyavarkaḷ, citamparam mīṉāṭci kallūriyil mutalvarāy viḷaṅkiya ṭākṭar u.vē.cāminātiyar pāl eṉṉi viṭuttut tolkāppiyac collatikārat teyvac ciliyār uri ēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyi vāṅkivara viṭuttārkaḷ; ṭākṭar. aiyaravarkaḷ eṉpāl avvēṭṭik koṭuttup paṭikkac collik kēṭṭu makiḻntu, "nīṅkaḷum ēṭṭil uḷḷa paḻiya nūlkaḷik kaṇṭu ārāycci ceyyalām; uṅkaḷiṭam uriya takutiyuḷatu" eṉṟu pārāṭṭiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭiya pārāṭṭum aṉpu moḻiyum eṉatu kuṟikkōḷi valiyuṟuttiṉa.
ivvāṟu tamiḻp pulami vāḻvi ēṟṟa kālattil, ṭākṭar aiyaravarkaḷiṉ ariya muyaṟciyāl, tamiḻar ceyta tavattiṉ naṟpayaṉākap puṟanāṉūṟu paḻiyavurivuṭaṉ veḷi vantiruntatu. āyiṉum, ataṉ piṟpakutikku uriyilli; pala pāṭṭukkaḷ citivuṟṟiruntaṉa. akkālattil tañci māvaṭṭattup paḷḷiyūrp pulavar tiru.kiruṭṭiṉacāmic cēṉināṭṭār avarkaḷiṭamirunta puṟanāṉūṟṟu ēṭṭiṉ tuṇi koṇṭum araciyalār veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷ ceppēṭukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭum muṟiyē citinta pāṭṭukkaḷiyum pāṭiya pulavar perumakkaḷiṉ peyar vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum ceppam ceytu uriyum ārāycci viḷakkamum eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭēṉ.
cilampum mēkaliyum eṉṟa kāppiyam iraṇṭaṉuḷ maṇimēkaliyiṉ piṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum putta tattuvak kūṟukaḷ viḷaṅkavilli eṉak kiviṭappaṭṭiruntatu. appōtu tirumali tiruppati tiruvēṅkaṭattāṉ kallūriyil yāṉ virivuriyāḷaṉākap paṇi purikiyil vaṭamoḻip pulavar kaḷāṉa tiru.pirapākara cāttiri, pāli moḻip pulavar tiru.aiyācāmi cāttiri ākiya iruvaruṭiya tuṇiyum ātaravum peṟṟup putta nūlkaḷi ārāyntu viṭupaṭṭirunta appakutikku virivāṉa ārāycciyum viḷakkamum eḻutiṉēṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟa nāvalar na.mu.vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭāravarkaḷ eḻutiya maṇimēkaliyuriyil avar viruppattiṟ koppac cērttu veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu.
kaṟṟōrkkum uṇartaṟkaritāyk kuṟippuriyōṭiyirunta patiṟṟuppattukku viḷakkamāṉa uriyillāmi oru kuṟiyāka iruntatu. tamiḻaṟiviṉkaṇ ārvam koṇṭa pulavarkaḷukkum māṇavarkaḷukkum annili periya iṭarppāṭu viḷittatu. ataṉāl ataṟkoru viḷakkavuriyum, ataṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum cēramaṉṉar varalāṟum vēṟu vēṟāka eḻutit tamiḻaṉṉikku iyaṉṟa paṇiyiṉic ceytēṉ. ivaṟṟil cēra maṉṉar varalāṟu oḻiya ēṉiyavaṟṟit teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṉar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
aiṅkuṟunūṟu eṉpatu eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟu; itaṉkaṇ aṭaṅkiya aiñnūṟu pāṭṭukkaḷuḷ oru cilavaṟṟiṟkup paḻiyōr oruvar eḻutiya kuṟippuri uṇṭu. ellāp pāṭṭiṟkum viḷakkamāṉa uri vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ataṉ mutaṟpakutiyāṉa marutam eṉṟa pakutiyi yāṉ eḻutiyavuriyuṭaṉ iḷamiyil eṉpāl tamiḻ payiṉṟu iṉṟu tamiḻaka aracu maṉṟat tuṇit talivarākac ciṟappuṟum pulavar kōvintaṉār veḷiyiṭṭār. piṉṉar annūluri muḻuvatiyum aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakam mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka  veḷiyiṭṭuḷatu.
aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattu ārāyccit tuṟi virivuriyāḷaṉāyp paṇipurintakāli, kālañceṉṟa pērāciriyar tiru.viyāpurippiḷḷi avarkaḷ utaviyāl naṟṟiṇi ēṭu oṉṟu kiṭikkap peṟṟēṉ. avvēṭṭit tantapōtu "itu maturit tamiḻc caṅka ēṭu; itaṉi naṉku ārāyntu uriyuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu tiru.piḷḷiyavarkaḷ paṇittārkaḷ. maturit tiyākarācar kallūriyil paṇipuriya vantapōtu, kallūri māṇavar oruvarālum, kallūri niṟuviya kalit tanti karu.muttu tiyākarācaṉār putalvar tiru.cuntaram ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷiṉ ātaravālum ēṭukaḷ kiṭikkappeṟṟu ārāyntatil mūvāyirattukkuk kuṟiyāta pāṭavēṟupāṭu kāṇappaṭṭamiyiṉ, avaṟṟic ceppam ceytu viḷakkavuri kaṇṭēṉ. tokuti yoṉṟukku iru nūṟu pāṭṭāka iru tokutikaḷāka ippōtu naṟṟiṇi veḷi varukiṟatu.
itaṟkiṭiyil, civa camaya ilakkiyat tuṟiyil, "irumpuk kaṭali" eṉṟu muṉṉōrāl collappaṭṭuk kiviṭap paṭṭirunta ñāṉāmirtam eṉṟa tamiḻ nūli ēṭukaḷ pala koṇṭu ārāyntu ataṉ paḻiyavurikku viḷakkak kuṟipput tantu oḻuṅku ceytēṉ. ataṉ arumiyi viyantu aṇṇā malip palkalik kaḻakattiṉar veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.
inta naṟṟiṇiyi ēṭu tēṭit tokuttuc cōrviṉṟi ārāyntu poruḷiṉmi vāyppiṉmi mutaliya iṭiyūṟum iṭiyīṭum tōṉṟi veḷiyiṭum paṇi iṭarppaṭātavāṟu poruḷutavi purinta naṉmakkaḷ palar. avaruḷ, kalit tanti, karu.muttu.tiyākarācaṉār, avaruṭiya tuṇiviyārāṉa cemmoḻic celvi tiru.irātā, tiyākarācaṉ ammiyār, poḷḷācci, vaṭalūr vaḷḷaliyuḷḷak kōyilil vittu vaḻipaṭum koṭivaḷḷal, nā.makāliṅkam avarkaḷ, tiruccirāp paḷḷi, tirukkuṟaḷvēḷ G.varatarācaṉār avaratu iḷaval kaṉakacapippiḷḷi, maturi vicālāṭci nūlāli atipar, paṇimoḻic celvar le.nārāyaṇaṉ ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷāvār.
innūl veḷiyīṭṭil pērūkkam koṇṭu iṉiya utavi puriyum celvar tēvakōṭṭi tiru.ki.karu.rāma. karu.cāminātaṉ ceṭṭiyār ciṟanta civaneṟiyāḷar. ceṉṉiyil uḷḷa aruṇā patippakattiṉ mutalvar. civaṉ tiruvaṭi maṟavāc cintiyum, tiṇṇiya tamiḻaṉpum uṭiyavar. iṅkē kūṟiya celvar palarkkum itaṉi aṉpōṭu acciṭṭutaviya maturi vijayā accaka mutalvar, tiru.kā.irāmaṉ avarkaṭkum centamiḻt tāyiṉ tiruvaruḷ mēṉmēlum peruka vēṇṭumeṉa ellām valla iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭiyip paravukiṉṟēṉ.
ivvakiyil pulami vāḻvil yāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kuṟikkōḷ virumpiyavāṟu niṟivuṟṟu eṉatu eḷiya uḷḷam makiḻvu koḷḷa aruḷ purinta maturi aṅkayaṟkaṇṇi
yōṭamarnta ālavāy aṇṇal tiruvaṭikaḷi vāḻtti vaṇaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
“āṭaka maturi aracē pōṟṟi
 kūṭal amarnta kurumaṇi pōṟṟi”
“avvi,”
  kānti nakar
    maturi
   16.9.1968				  oḷavi. turicāmi

naṟṟiṇi
mūlamum viḷakkavuriyum
201.paraṇar
talimicāl paṇpum ceyalumuṭiya talimakaṉ malic cāraṟ pūmpoḻiṟkuḷ taṉittuc ceṉṟu aṅkē poḻil viḷiyāṭṭu viruppāṟ pōntirunta talimakaḷik kaṇṭu kātaluṟṟāṉ. avaḷuṭiya iṉiya nōkkattālum piṟa kuṟippuk kaḷālum avaḷ uḷḷattum taṉpāl kātal tōṉṟiṉami kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟu avaḷuṭaṉ iṉpa uṟavukoḷḷa muyaṉṟāṉ. muṭivil iruvar  karuttum kātalāl naṭpuṟṟu oṉṟupaṭṭaṉa. ciṟitupōtil avaḷ, avaṇ pōnta āyamakaḷiruṭaṉ kūṭic ceṉṟāḷ. muṉṉāḷ uḷatāya naṭpi vaṟpuṟuttaṟku maṟunāḷ avaṉ appoḻiṟkuc ceṉṟāṉāka, avaṉippōla avaḷum avaṇ pōntu taṉ kātal nalatti uṟuticeytāḷ. itu kāṟum kaṇṭaṟiyāta puttuṇarcci talikkoṇṭa talimakaṉ avaḷi aritiṟpirintu ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉ uḷḷak kōyilil avaḷum, avaḷ uḷḷattil avaṉum iṭam peṟṟaṉar. peṇmi pōla āṇmi, taṉ uḷḷat tuṇarcciyi veḷippaṭāvakiyil maṟikka vēṇṭiya kaṭappāṭuṭiya taṉṟu; uḷḷattut tōṉṟiya uṇarvi uruvākkutaṟkuriya muyaṟciyum toḻilum āṇ makaṉā yiṉāṉ mēṟkōṭaṟku ipparanta ulaku pōtiya vāypput tantuḷḷatu. ataṉāl avaṉ taṉ karuttit taṉ maṉattukkiṉiya naṇpaṉukku aṟivittāṉ. peṇṇoruttiyiṉ viḻivalip paṭṭup pētuṟuvatu āṇmikkup perumi taruvataṉṟu eṉa naṇpaṉ avaṉik kaḻaṟiṉāṉ. piṉpu talimakaṉ uḷḷattiṉ uṟuti kaṇṭa naṇpaṉ, talimakaṉ kuṟitta iṭattukkut tāṉē taṉittuc ceṉṟu talimakaḷi nērē nōkkiṉāṉ. talimakaḷiṉ uruvum nalamum tiruvum cemmiyum kaṇṭatum, avaṉatu uḷḷamum peruviyappeytiṟṟu. viṇmīṉ naṭuvaṇ taṇmati pōlat talimakaḷ iyaṅkuvatiyum avaḷ tantiyiṉ cālpiyum piṟa celvacciṟappukkaḷiyum kaṇṭu talimakaṉpāṟpōntu, “niṉ uḷḷaṅ koṇṭa talimakaḷ pāl viḷaṅkum talimip paṇpum ceyalum nōkka avaḷip peṟutal ariteṉṟē yāṉ karutukiṉṟēṉ;ātalāl avaḷporuṭṭu muyalvatu naṉṟaṉṟu’’ eṉṟāṉ. atukēṭṭa talimakaṉ muṟuvalittu “naṇpa, kuṉṟakkuṟavaṉ makaḷāṉa attali makaḷ peṟutaṟ kariyaḷ; ariya kāvalaminta cūḻniliyiṉāḷ; avaḷ niṉ vēṇṭukōḷi ēṟkamāṭṭāta iḷiyaḷ; avaḷi niṉittal vēṇṭā eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟāy; nī kūṟuvaṉa aṉittum uṇmiyē; āyiṉum uruvum nalaṉum paṭitta avaḷ, kollik kuṭavariyiṉkaṇ niṟkum poṟpāvi, peruṅkāṟṟu perumaḻi kaṭuveyil mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟitum aciviṉṟip polintu viḷaṅkutal pōla, eṉ neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟṟu viṭṭāḷ; iṉi avaḷ ataṉiṉ nīṅkāḷ” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
talimakaṉuṭiya ivvuriyiṉkaṇ, iḷami yuḷḷattuk kātaluṇarvu paṟṟit tōṉṟum peṇmiyuru, iḷiñaṉ oruvaṉ niṉiviyum colliyum taṉvari niṟuttip piṇittu niṟkum tiṟam viḷaṅkak kaṇṭu viyanta paraṇar ataṉi ip pāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
maliyuṟi kuṟavaṉ kātal maṭamakaḷ
peṟalaruṅ kuriyaḷ aruṅkaṭik kāppiṉaḷ
colletir1 koḷḷā iḷiyaḷ aṉiyaḷ
uḷḷal kūṭā teṉpōy2 maṟṟum
cevvērp palaviṉ payaṅkeḻu kollit
teyvam kākkum tītutīr neṭuṅkōṭ
ṭavveḷ ḷaruvik kuṭavari yakattuk
kālporu tiṭippiṉum kataḻuṟi kaṭukiṉum
urumuṭaṉ ṟeṟiyiṉum ūṟupala tōṉṟiṉum
perunilam kiḷariṉum tirunala vuruviṉ 
māyā iyaṟkip1 pāviyiṉ 
pōtal ollāḷeṉ2 neñcat tāṉē. 
itu kaḻaṟiya pāṅkaṟkut talimakaṉ urittatu.
uri : 
maliyuṟi kuṟavaṉ kātal maṭamakaḷ - maliyiṭattē  vāḻum  kuṟavaṉuṭiya aṉpum iḷamiyumuṭiya makaḷ; peṟalaruṅ kuriyaḷ - peṟutaṟ kariyavaḷ; aruṅkaṭik kāppiṉaḷ neruṅkutaṟku ariya kāvaliṭattuḷḷāḷ; colletir koḷḷā iḷiyaḷ - nī kūṟum coṟkaḷi etirēṟṟuk koḷḷalākāta iḷamiyuṭiyaḷ; aṉiyaḷ uḷḷal kūṭātu eṉpōy- appeṟṟiyavaḷi virumpi niṉittal kūṭātu eṉṟu kaḻaṟukiṉṟa naṇpaṉē; cevvērp palaviṉ payam keḻu kolli - cevviya vērppalāviṉ paḻaṅkaḷ poruntiya kollimaliyil; teyvam kākkum tītutīr neṭuṅkōṭṭu - teyvattāṟ kākkappaṭutalāl kuṟṟa millāta neṭiya muṭiyil; avveḷ aruvik kuṭavari yakattu - aḻakiya veḷḷaruviyoṭu kūṭiya mēlip pakutiyil; kāl porutu iṭippiṉum - kāṟṟu mōtit tākkiṉālum; kataḻuṟi kaṭukiṉum - perumaḻi kaṭukip peyyumāyiṉum; urum uṭaṉṟu eṟiyiṉum - iṭi muḻaṅkit tākkiṉālum; ūṟupala tōṉṟiṉum - iṉṉōraṉṉa pala iṭiyūṟukaḷ vantālum; peru nilam kiḷariṉum - periya nilam naṭuṅki ṉālum; tirunala uruviṉ māyā iyaṟkip pāviyiṉ - kaṇṭār virumpum uruvap polivu keṭāta iyalpiṉa tākiya pāviyip pōla; eṉ neñcattāṉ pōtal ollāḷ - eṉ neñciṉiṉṟum nīṅkuvā ḷallaḷ, ākaliṉ avaḷi yāṉ evvāṟu maṟappēṉ e.ṟu.
kuṟavaṉmakaḷ peṟalaruṅ kuriyaḷ, colletir  koḷḷā iḷiyaḷ, aṉiyaḷi uḷḷal kūṭātu eṉpōy, kollik kuṭa variyakattu iṭippiṉum, kaṭukiṉum, eṟiyiṉum, tōṉṟiṉum,kiḷariṉum, uruviṉ māyā iyaṟkip pāviyiṉ, neñcattāṉ pōtal ollāḷ, ākavē avaḷi yāṉ evvāṟu maṟappēṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. kuri, acinili. kaṭi, mikuti. colletirkoḷḷal, collappaṭum coṟ poruḷi ēṟṟut takuvaṉa eṇṇiyurikkum maṉavuṇar vuṭimi mēṟṟu. uḷḷal, virumpi niṉittal; kātalit talumām vērppalā, palāviṉvaki. tītu iṉmikku ētu teyvam kāttal. kollimaliyiṉ mēlippakuti kuṭavari eṉappaṭṭatu. kataḻuṟi, virinta maḻi. eḷitiṟkiḷarāta perumi yuṭimipaṟṟip perunilam eṉṟār. tiru. kaṇṭōr virumpum aḻaku. neñcattāṉ eṉṟaviṭattu, aintāvataṉkaṇ mūṉṟāvatu mayaṅkiṟṟu eṉka. maṟṟu, viṉimāṟṟu. ummi iciniṟi.
maliyuṟiyum kuṟavaṉ aritiṟ peṟṟa talimakaḷ eṉṟaṟku maliyuṟi kuṟavaṉ kātal maṭamakaḷ eṉṟum, kuṟavaṉ nayantu koṭinērntā laṉṟip peṟalariyaḷ eṉṟaṟkuk kātal maṭamakaḷ peṟalaruṅ kuriyaḷ eṉṟum. avaṉi aṟiyātu kaḷaviṟ ceṉṟu avaḷik kāṇṭal aritu eṉṟaṟku aruṅ kaṭik kāppiṉaḷ eṉṟum, kāval kaṭantu orukāṟ ceṉṟu avaḷik kāṇiṉum nām kuṟippiṉ uṇarttum coṟkaḷik kēṭṭu uṇarntu maṟumāṟṟam tarum attuṇi maṉavuṇarvu uṇṭākāta iḷamippaṇpiṉaḷ eṉpāṉ colletir koḷḷā iḷiyaḷ eṉṟum appeṟṟiyavaḷi niṉittalum peṟaṟku muyaṟalum payaṉil ceyalām eṉpāṉ aṉiyaḷ uḷḷal kūṭātu eṉṟum pāṅkaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. ittuṇiyum pāṅkaṉ kūṟṟu; itaṉi maṟuppatu karuttākaliṉ eṉpōy eṉat talimakaṉ koṇṭeṭuttu moḻintāṉ. “kūṟṟavaṇ iṉmi yiṉ ’’1 vanta koṇṭukūṟṟaṉṟu. palaviṉ paḻam poruntiyatu eṉṟum, teyvaṅ kāttalāl tītu tīrntatu eṉṟum, kolli maliyic ciṟappittāṉ, taṉ muyaṟciyum payaṉviḷivikkum eṉṟum, teyvam kāttalāl tītiṉṟā meṉṟum talimakaṉ kuṟippāl pāṅkaṉit teruṭṭutaṟku. māyā iyaṟkip pāviyiṉ pōtal ollāḷ eṉ neñcattāṉē eṉṟatu, avaḷi maṟattaṟku muyalvati viṭuttup peṟaṟku muyalvatē ceyaṟpālatu eṉat talimakaṉ pāṅkaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉām. itu kēṭṭup pāṅkaṉkūṭṭattukku uriya muyaṟci mēṟkoḷ vāṉāvatu payaṉ.

202. pāli pāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō
tammiṟṟām taṉittuk kaṇṭu kātaluṟavu koṇṭu kaḷavu
neṟikkaṇ oḻuki varuṅkāl, talimakaṉ talimakaḷi nēriya muṟiyil varintu koḷḷalākātavāṟu cūḻnili iṭiyūṟu ceytatu. talimakaṉiyiṉṟi imippōtum uyirvāḻtal amiyāta aḷavil talimakaḷ uḷḷattiṟ kātal muṟukip perukiṟṟu. atu kaṇṭa talimakaṉ taṉṉuṭaṉ koṇṭu celvāṉāyiṉaṉ. kālippōtil talimakaṭkuriya nāṭṭiṉ ellikkaṇṇuḷḷa kāṭṭiṉuḷ pukuntu naṇpakal eytumuṉ kāṭṭiṉiyum kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉar. veyiliṉ vemmi teriyā vāṟu kāṭṭiṭit tōṉṟiya kāṭcikaḷum peru maraṅkaḷ tanta niḻalum avarkaṭku iṉpam ceytaṉa. kāṭṭiṉ naṭuvē ceṟinta pacuntaḻi pōrtta maliyoṉṟu niṉṟatu. ammaliyiṉ piḷavukaḷi lellām iruḷpaṭat taḻitta maraṅkaḷum ceṭi koṭikaḷum mikkiruntaṉa. maravakikaḷuḷ kōṅka maraṅkaḷ civanta pūkkaḷit tāṅki viḷakkukaḷip pōloḷirntaṉa. orupuṟam kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi yoṉṟu taṉṉoṭu poruta puliyik koṉṟu, pulāl nāṟum taṉ maruppiṭattē muttukkaḷ olippa maṟam ciṟantu ceṉṟu, uyarntu tōṉṟiya nilappakutiyil niṉṟa vēṅkimarattik kaṇṭu ataṉ aṭippakutiyit takarttuk kīḻē taḷḷi, taṉpāl vantu kūṭiya piṭikkum ataṉ kaṉṟukkum vēṅkiyiṉ pacuntaḻiyik kavaḷamākat tantu puṟantantu niṉṟatu. kaḷiṟṟi yāṉiyiṉ ceyaliyum kārttiki viḷakkup pōla malarntu tōṉṟum kōṅkamarac ceṟivukaḷiyum tali makaṉ  talimakaṭkuk kāṭṭi, “kuṟumakaḷē kaḷiṟu piṭiyiyum kaṉṟiṉiyum pēṇip puṟantantu niṟkum maliyit taṉṉakattē koṇṭu, malarnta pūkkaḷit tāṅkiyoḷirum kōṅku maraṅkaḷi yuṭiyatāṉa ikkāṭu niṉ tantik kuriyatu; itaṉik kāṇpāyāka” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
talimakaṉuṭiya ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, kaḷiṟṟiyāṉiyiṉ ceyaṉmēl vittut tāṉ pakikaṭintu maṉiyaṟam puriyum māṇpum takutiyum uṭiyaṉ eṉpatum, kōṅkam kārttiki viḷakkup pōlat tōṉṟum  kāṭcimēl vittu talimakaḷ taṉ māṇpuṭik kaṟpāl maṉikku viḷakkāyt tikaḻvaḷ eṉpatum, viḷaṅkutal kaṇṭa peruṅkaṭuṅkō ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇ avaṟṟit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
puliporac civanta1 pulavunā ṟiruṅkōṭ
ṭolipal muttam ārppa valiciṟantu
vaṉcuvaṟ parāri murukkik kaṉṟoṭu
maṭappiṭi taḻīiya taṭakki vēḻam
tēṉcey peruṅkiḷi iriya vēṅkip
poṉpuri kavaḷam puṟantaru pūṭṭum 
māmali viṭarakam kaviik kāṇvarak
kaṇṭiciṉ vāḻiyō kuṟumakaḷ nunti 
aṟumīṉ keḻīiya aṟamcey tiṅkaṭ
celcuṭar neṭuṅkoṭi pōlap
palpūṅ kōṅkam aṇinta kāṭē.
iḵtu, uṭaṉ pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.3
uri:
puliporac civanta pulavunāṟu iruṅkōṭṭu - puliyik koṉṟataṉāl civanta pulālnāṟum periya kompiṉkaṇ; olipal muttam ārppa - uṇṭākiya palavākiya muttukkaḷ olikka; vali ciṟantu - vaṉkaṇmi mikuntu; vaṉcuval parāri murukki-valiya mēṭṭu nilattil niṉṟa parutta aṭippakutiyit takarttut taḷḷi; kaṉṟoṭu maṭappiṭi taḻīiya taṭakki vēḻam - kaṉṟuṭaṉēyuḷḷa iḷaminalam paṭitta taṉ piṭi yāṉiyoṭu kūṭiya periya kiyiyuṭiya kaḷiṟu; tēṉ cey peruṅkiḷi iriya - tēṉi yīṭṭutaṟkuk kūṭiyirunta vaṇṭiṉattiṉ periya kūṭṭam nīṅka ōṭṭi; vēṅkip poṉpuri kavaḷam - vēṅkiyiṉ taḻiyum  pūvum kalanta poṉpōlum uṇavukkavaḷatti; puṟam tarupu ūṭṭum - aṉpōṭu uṇpikkum ; māmali viṭarakam kavii - periya maliyiṭattup piḷaviṭaṅkaḷit taṉṉakattēkoṇṭu; kāṇvarak kaṇṭiciṉ - aḻakuṇṭākat tōṉṟuvatik kāṇpāyāka; vāḻi -; kuṟumakaḷ - iḷiyavaḷē; nunti - niṉ tantikkuriya; aṟumīṉ keḻīiya aṟañcey tiṅkaḷ - kārttiki mīṉkaḷoṭu kūṭiya aṟañceytaṟ kaminta muḻuttiṅkaḷ viḷaṅkum nāḷiṉkaṇ; celcuṭar neṭuṅ koṭi pōla - variciyākac cellukiṉṟa oḷi poruntiya neṭiya viḷakkukaḷiṉ oḻuṅkupōla; kōṅkam aṇinta kāṭu - palavākiya pūkkaḷit tāṅkiniṟkum kōṅkamaraṅkaḷāl aṇipeṟṟu viḷaṅkum kāṭṭiṉi e.ṟu.
kuṟumakaḷ, vāḻi; aṟumīṉ keḻīiya tiṅkaḷ celcuṭar neṭuṅkoṭi pōlak kōṅkam aṇinta nunti kāṭu, māmali viṭarakam kaviik kāṇvarak kaṇṭiciṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. puliyoṭu porutu ataṉik koṉṟamiyāl kōṭu ataṉ kuruti tōyntu civantiruttal paṟṟip puliporac civanta pulavunāṟu iruṅkōṭu eṉṟār. yāṉi maruppil muttu uṇṭām eṉpatu koṇṭu olipal muttam ārppa eṉṟār. olittal - uṇṭātal. vali vaṉkaṇmi. parāri, parutta aṭimaram. vēṅkimaram pūniṟintu vēṅkippuliyi niṉippittaliṉ, vēḻam ataṉit takartta teṉṟār. “koṭu vari tākki veṉṟa varuttamoṭu , neṭuvari maruṅkiṉ tuñcum yāṉi, naṉaviṟṟāṉ ceytatu maṉatta tākaliṉ, kaṉaviṟ  kaṇṭu katumeṉa verīip, putuvatāka malarnta vēṅkiyi, atuveṉa uṇarntataṉ aṇinalam murukkip, pē'82 muṉpiṉ taṉciṉam taṇintu1” eṉpatu kāṇka. vaṉṉilattu niṉṟatāyiṉum vēṅki maratti yāṉi valiciṟantamiyāl murukkiṟṟeṉa aṟika. pakittu ūṟu ceyvāṉ pōnta vēṅkippuliyi veṉṟu ataṉi yokkum  vēṅkimarattiyum takarttuc ciṉantaṇinta vēḻam kaṉṟoṭu kūṭiyuṟiyum  taṉ maṭappiṭiyit taḻuvi aṉpuceyyum eṉpār, kaṉṟoṭu maṭappiṭi taḻīiya taṭakki vēḻam vēṅkip poṉpuri kavaḷam puṟantarupu ūṭṭum eṉṟār. viṭarakam eṉṟatu īṇṭu malikaṭku iṭiyē uḷḷa piḷappiṭam; itaṉip paḷḷattākku eṉṟal ikkāla vaḻakku. kavii - kavavu, akattiṭal. ō acinili. aṟumīṉ - kārttiki. kārttiki nāḷum  muḻumatiyum kūṭiya nāḷ kārttikit tiṅkaḷ; maṟunāḷ toṭaṅkuvatu mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷ. aḵtu āniri nāḷum muḻumatiyum kūṭiya nāḷoṭu niṟivuṟum. muḻuttiṅkaḷil aṟañ ceytalum nōṉpu mēṟ kōṭalum marapātaliṉ muḻumati  nāḷi aṟañcey tiṅkaḷ eṉṟār. ikkālattut tīpāvaḷip putunāḷip pōlac caṅka kālattuk kārttiki muḻuttiṅkaḷ tamiḻarvāḻvil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. piṟkālattē pallavar āṭciyil paḻantamiḻk koḷkikaḷi māṟṟi vaṭa nūṉ muṟiyil amikkavēṇṭu meṉṟa muyaṟci maṟimukamākat tōṉṟip  piṟkālap pāṇṭiya cōḻa vēntar kālattil urutteriyāmal vaḷarntu, vicayanakara vēntarāṭciyil veḷippaṭa muṟpōntu, ūrppeyar, makkaṭ peyar, kōyilkaḷiluḷḷa iṟivarkaḷ peyar yāvum vaṭa moḻippaṭuttiṟṟu; kōyil nikaḻccikaḷ camayanikaḻccikaḷ mutaliya palavum vaṭamoḻip peyarpeṟṟaṉa; viḻākkaḷiṉ peyarum kālamum māṟiṉa; airōppiyar kālattil makkaṭ peyarellām peritum vaṭamoḻiyāyiṉa; kōyil nikaḻcci kaḷum viḻākkaḷum yāvum māṟiṉa . muḻuvatum tamiḻē  nilaviya kōyilkaḷilum camaya niliyaṅkaḷilum araciyal vaḻakkiyal kaḷilum tirumaṇam mutaliya camutāyattuṟikaḷilum vaṭa moḻiyum vaṭanūṟ koḷkikaḷum naṉku paravit tamiḻaratu toṉmiyi maṟittu iṉṟu varalāṟṟuc ciṟappu ētumillāta oru putuccamutāyam pōlak kāṭcitarum niliyil māṟṟi veṟṟi kaṇṭatuṭaṉ, paḻantamiḻar taṅkaḷ nāṭṭu naṉmakkaṭku vaḻaṅkip pārāṭṭiya kāviti mutaliya peyarkaḷic cātip peyarākki, ataṉiyuṭiyārit tāḻnta makkaḷākkip paḻan tamiḻc coṟkaḷi eḷḷi nakiyāṭutaṟkēṟṟa maṉaniliyit tamiḻariṭattē uṇṭākkiviṭṭaṉa. mēṉāṭṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl varalāṟṟuṇarvu ciṟitu peṟṟatum, tamiḻar inta iḻukkiṉi eṇṇi eriyeḻa nōkkat talippaṭṭārāka, araciyal cūḻniliyum camutāyap poruḷātāra māṟutalum avaratu muyaṟciyi oṭukkiviṭṭaṉa. koṭi, oḻuṅku. kōṅkam pū, cuṭarpōl tikaḻum eṉpar; “kōṅkiṉ putumalar kiviṭu cuṭariṉ tōṉṟum’’1 eṉṟu varum.  maḻikāl nīṅkiya māka vicumpiṟ, kuṟumuyal maṟuniṟaṅkiḷara mati niṟintu, aṟumīṉ cēru makaliruḷ naṭunāḷ, maṟuku viḷakkuṟuttu māli tūkkip, paḻaviṟaṉ mūtūrp palaruṭaṉ tuvaṉṟiya viḻavu’’2 eṉa nakkīrar ikkārttiki viḻāvik kūṟutal kāṇka. 
iravup pōtil talimakaḷik koṇṭutalikkaḻiyum  talimakaṉ, pakaṟpōtil kāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē avaḷōṭu celluṅkāl, vaḻivaruttam tōṉṟātavāṟu collāṭa luṟṟu, akkāṭṭiṉkaṇ vēḻam taṉ kaṉṟuṭiya maṭappiṭikku vēṅkip poṉpuri kavaḷam tantu puṟantaruvatu kāṭṭuvāṉ, yāṉiyiṉ kōṭu civantu tōṉṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam kūṟaluṟṟup puliporac civanta pulavunāṟu iruṅkōṭu eṉṟum, atu piṉpu vēṅki marattit takarttamikku ētu, vaṉkaṇmi mikuntami eṉpāṉ, valiciṟantu vaṉcuvaṟ parāri murukki eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. cuval, mēṭṭu nilam. vaṉcuvaṟ parāri yeṉṟāṉ, meṉṉila māyiṉ vēṅkiyi vērōṭē vīḻttiyirukkum  eṉṟaṟku. itaṉāṟ payaṉ, vēḻam ciṉam taṇintamivatu. vēḻam puliyōṭu porutapōtu maṭappiṭi taṉ kaṉṟoṭu ceṉṟu kāppuṭiya māmali viṭarakattē oṭuṅki yirunta teṉṟaṟku, puṟantaru pūṭṭum māmali viṭarakam eṉa muṭittāṉ. vēṅkimaram putuppū malarntu vaṇṭiṉam tēṉuṇṭu pāṭum nalamuṭiya teṉṟaṟkut tēṉcey peruṅkiḷi iriya eṉṟum, vēṅkiyiṉ taḻiyum pūvum kuḻitta kavaḷattip poṉ cey kavaḷam eṉṟum ciṟappittuk kūṟiṉāṉ. ivvāṟu vēḻattiṉ viṉi yāṇmi kūṟiyatu. yāṉum ittakiya viṉiyāṇmiyāl vīṟeyti avviṉiyiṭattup peṟum poruḷkoṇṭu niṉṉip puṟantaruvēṉ eṉat tali

makaḷit teḷivitta vāṟu eṉka. eṉavē, niṉṉik kāppēṉ eṉpatu collāmaṟ colliyavāṟāyiṟṟu. nuntiyatu kāṭṭil kōṅkam palavākiya pūkkaḷit tāṅkik kārttiki viḷakkup pōlat tikaḻumeṉṟāṉ, nīyum niṉ maṉikkaṇ palvakic ciṟappukkaḷāl naṉku viḷakkam peṟuvāy eṉpatu vaṟpuṟuttaṟku. itaṉāl talivi maṉam makiḻvāḷāvatu payaṉ.
203. ulōccaṉār
kaḷavuk kātalāṟ piṇippuṇṭu oruvari yoruvar kuṟiyiṉ
kaṇ talippeytu iṉpuṟṟu varukiyil, allakuṟiyālum vēṟupala iṭiyīṭukaḷālum talimakkaḷatu kūṭṭam iṭiyaṟavu paṭuvatāyiṟṟu. āyiṉum, talimakaṉ uḷḷam taḷarāmal kaḷaviyē virumpiyoḻukiṉāṉ. taliviyuḷḷattu nilaviya kātal ciṟantu vaḷarvatu karutit talivaṉ avvariviṉi nīṭṭittu vantāṉ. inniliyil avaṉ oru nāḷ iruḷmālip pōtil talivi maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟamāka vantu niṉṟāṉāka, ataṉit tōḻi yuṇarntu, talimakaṉ niṉṟa pakutiyil avaṉ aṟiyāvakiyil talimakaḷoṭu ceṉṟu niṉṟu, tāṉ kūṟuvatu avaṉ ceviyiṟ paṭum aḷavil taliviyoṭu collāṭaluṟṟāḷ: “tōḻi, mutaṟkaṇ nām nammūrk kāṉaṟcōliyiṭattē talimakaṉit talippeytu naṭpuṟṟōm; annaṭpup peruki oruvari yoruvar orunāḷ kā'82tu kaḻiyiṉum uyiruytal ariteṉṉu māṟu ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ataṉiyaṟiyāmal, orunāḷ nam kātalar tēr nam mūrp pakkal  vantatu koṇṭu ivvūr pēralar eṭuttu mīṇṭum avarik kā'82tavāṟu nammi illiṉkaṇ naṉku ceṟittuviṭṭatu; atuvēyumaṉṟi, ippōḻtu nampāl uḷatāya melivu kaṇṭu neñcu varuntutaliyum ceykiṉṟatu; iḵtu eṉṉākumō eṉṟu yāṉ añcukiṉṟēṉ ’’  eṉṟu moḻintāḷ. tōḻiyiṉatu ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talimakaṭkum talimakaṟkum kāṉaṟ cōliyiṭattu nikaḻnta kūṭṭattāl uḷatāya kātal ciṟantu, avaṉi yiṉṟi uyir vāḻta lamiyāta aḷavil talivipāl muṟukiyiruppatum, ataṉit teruṇṭu talimakaṉ varital vēṇṭum eṉpatum, nīṭṭittal tīteṉ patum viḷaṅkutal kaṇṭa ulōccaṉār avaḷuṭiya coṟ kaḷi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittu aḻakuṟap pāṭukiṉṟār. 
muḻaṅkutiri koḻīiya mūri ekkart
taṭantāṭ ṭāḻi1 muḷḷili neṭuṅkōṭ
ṭakamaṭaṟ potuḷiya2 mukimutir paviḻnta
kōṭuvārn taṉṉa3 tōṭupoti veṇpū
eṟitiri utittaliṉ poṅkit tātucōrpu
ciṟukuṭip pākkattu maṟukupulāl maṟukkum
4maṇaṅkamaḻ kāṉal iyintanaṅ kēṇmi 
orunāḷ 5kaḻiyiṉum uyvari 6teṉṉātu 
kataḻpari neṭuntēr 7varavāṇ ṭaḻuṅkac
ceytataṉ tappal aṉṟiyum 
uyavuppuṇarn taṉṟiv vaḻuṅkal ūrē
itu talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāy varivu kaṭāyatu. 
uri: 
muḻaṅkutiri koḻīiya mūri ekkar - muḻaṅkukiṉṟa kaṭal alikaḷāl koḻittiṭappaṭṭa periya maṇalmēṭṭil niṟkum; taṭantāḷ tāḻi muḷḷili neṭuṅkōṭṭu - periya tāḷiyuṭiya  tāḻiyiṉ muḷ poruntiya ilikaḷi yuṭiya neṭiya kiḷiyiṉkaṇ pūtta; akamaṭal potuḷiya mukimutirpu aviḻnta - maṭaliṉ akattē tōṉṟiya arumpu muṟṟutalāl muṟukkaviḻnta; kōṭu vārntaṉṉa tōṭu poti veṇpū - caṅku nīṇṭāṟpōṉṟa tōṭu mūṭṭap paṭṭa veṇmiyāṉa pū; eṟitiri utittaliṉ - kāṟṟāl eṟiyappaṭṭa kaṭalalikaḷ mōtutalāl; poṅkit tātu cōrpu - citintu tātu utirntu; ciṟukuṭip pākkattu maṟuku pulāl maṟukkum - ciṟukuṭip pākkattut terukkaḷil paravum pulāl  nāṟṟatti māṟṟum; maṇamkamaḻ kāṉal iyinta   nam kēṇmi - naṟumaṇam kamaḻum kāṉaṟcōliyiṭattu uṇṭākiya nam kātaluṟavu ciṟantamiyāl; oru nāḷ kaḻi yiṉum uyvaritu eṉṉātu-- orunāḷ kāṇātoḻiyiṉum nām uyir uytal aritu eṉṟu niṉiyāmal;kataḻpari neṭuntēr varavu  - virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya kutiri pūṭṭiya nam kātalar tēriṉatu  varavu; āṇṭu aḻuṅkac ceyta taṉ tappal aṉṟiyum - tāṉ eṭuttut tūṟṟiya alarāl avar ūriṭattē tavirac ceyta tavaṟṟōṭamiyātu; ivvaḻuṅkal ūr - inta āravārattiyuṭiya ūr; uyavup puṇarntaṉṟu - ivviṭattu  nam pāl uḷatākiya melivu kaṇṭu varuttam mēliṭṭuk koṇṭu iraṅkāniṉṟa tākalāṉ iṉi eṉṉākumō eṉṟu añcukiṉṟēṉ e.ṟu.
kāṉal iyinta nam kēṇmiyāl nām uyvaritu eṉṉātu, neṭuntēr varavu, āṇṭu aḻuṅkac ceytataṉ tappa laṉṟiyum, ivvaḻuṅkalūr uyavup puṇarntaṉṟu; ākalāṉ iṉi eṉṉākumō eṉṟu añcukiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. ekkart tāḻi neṭuṅkōṭṭu akamaṭaṟ potuḷiya muki mutirpu aviḻnta veṇpū poṅkit tātu cōrpu, pulāl maṟukkum kāṉal eṉa iyiyum. ciṟantamiyāl eṉa oru coṟpeytu koḷka. ākalāṉ iṉi eṉpatu mutalāyiṉa kuṟip peccam. uyavu, varuttam. cuḷakukoṇṭu koḻittāṟpōlak kaṭalalikaḷāl nuṇmaṇal otukkap paṭutalpaṟṟik koḻīiya ekkar eṉṟum, uyariya maṇalmēṭu eṉṟaṟku mūriekkar eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. taṭantāḷ: uriccoṟ puṇarcci: tāḻiyiṉ ili muḷḷuṭimipaṟṟi muḷḷili yeṉappaṭṭatu. puṟamaṭal muḷḷuṭi ilikaḷ pōṟaliṉ akamaṭal kūṟiṉār. muki mutirntālaṉṟi itaḻ viriyāmiyiṉ muki mutirpu aviḻnta eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu. kōṭu, caṅku. vārtal, nīḷutal. ciṟukuṭip pākkam -  neytal nilattu ūr. maṟuku - teru. maṟuttal īṇṭu māṟṟutal mēṟṟu. tappal, tavaṟu.
talimakaḷ uḷḷattu nilaviya kātal ciṟattal vēṇṭik kaḷaviṉkaṇ kāṉaṟ cōlikaḷiṭattu aminta kuṟiyiṭattē pōntu, talimakaṉ talippey toḻukiya tiṟatti, maṇaṅ kamaḻ kāṉal iyinta namkēṇmi eṉṟum. tāṉ virumpi yāṅkuk kātal ciṟantami yuṇarttutaṟku  oru nāḷ kaḻiyiṉum uyvaritu eṉṟum, ataṉi yuṇarāmal oruvaḻittaṇappu mutaliya pirivi iṭippeytu kaḷavu nīṭṭittami paṟṟi, uyvari teṉṉātu kataḻpari neṭuntēr varavu āṇṭu aḻuṅka eṉṟum, talimakaṉ tēr varavu aḻuṅkiyataṟku ūravar eṭutta alar kāraṇameṉak karutit talivi vēṟupaṭṭāḷā kaliṉ ūrmē lēṟṟi, tērvaravu āṇṭu aḻuṅkac ceytataṉ tappal eṉṟum, nōy ceytamiyē yaṉṟi, nōy kaṇṭu iraṅki ūr mutupeṇṭir veṟi mutalāyiṉa eṭuttal  kūṟumāṟu tōṉṟa uyavup puṇarntaṉṟu iv vaḻuṅkal ūr eṉṟum, iṉiyum varivu nīṭṭippiṉ ivaḷ uyirvāḻāḷ eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. ekkart tāḻiyiṉ veṇpūt tiriyutittalāṟ poṅkit tātu corintu pākkattu maṟukiṉkaṇ uṇṭāya pulālnāṟṟatti māṟṟutal pōla, nīyum kūṭṭam iṭiyīṭu paṭutalāl kiḷarnteḻuntu variporuḷic corintu ivaḷi varintu koḷḷumāṟṟāl  ūravar eṭutta alarkkuk kāraṇa mākiya ivaḷatu mēṉi melivip pōkkutal vēṇṭumeṉat tōḻi kūṟiya tāka uḷḷuṟi yuvamam koḷka. talimakaṉ virintu varivu mēṟkoḷvāṉāvatu payaṉ.
204. ammaḷḷaṉār
accuppiratikaḷil ivar peyar maḷḷaṉār eṉṟu kāṇap paṭiṉum, ēṭukaḷil ammaḷḷaṉār eṉak kāṇap paṭṭamiyiṉ  atuvē koḷḷappaṭṭatu. 
talimakaṉ, taṉ pōlat talimi māṇpuṭiya tali makaḷit taṉimiyiṟ kaṇṭu kātaluṟavu koṇṭa piṉṉar iṭantalippaṭṭum pāṅkaṉvaḻic ceṉṟum avvuṟavi vaṟpuṟuttikkoṇṭu akkātal uyirp piṇippāyc ciṟattal vēṇṭik kaḷavoḻukkatti mēṟkoḷḷa niṉintāṉ. ataṟku uriya tuṇiyākupavaḷ taliviyiṉ uyirttōḻi yākum. avaḷuṭiya naṭpiṉip peṟṟālaṉṟit talimakaṉ talimakaḷit tāṉ virumpiyavāṟu taṉittut talippeyta leṉpatu iyalāta oṉṟām. talimakaḷum  taṉakku uṟutuṇi yāy nilavum taṉ uyirttōḻiyit takka kuṟippukkaḷāl talimakaṟkuk  kāṭṭa, avaṉum avaḷik kaṇṭukoṇṭāṉ. talimakaṉukkum taṉakkum uḷatākiya kātaluṟaviṉi t tali makaḷ  taṉ uyirttōḻikkum  urittilaḷ. taṉ maṉattil tōṉṟiya kātaluṟavi veḷiyē kaṇṇaṉṉa kēḷirkkum kāṭṭāta tiṇmi peṇmikku iyalpu; kātaluṟavi veḷip paṭac ceytu kaṭimaṇattāl ulakaṟiyac ceyvatu āṇmiyiṉ aṟamum kaṭaṉum paṇiyumākum. ataṉāl, tōḻikkut tali makkaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟiya uṟavu ciṟitum teriyātu; tali makaṉiyum avaḷ aṟiyāḷ. inniliyil  talimakaṉ tali makaḷuṟiyum cūḻalukkuc ceṉṟu orumuṟikkup paṉmuṟi tōḻiyōṭu collāṭi avaḷuṭiya naṭpip peṟa muyal vaṉ. tōḻiyum talimakaḷiyotta aṟivum aṟamum peṇmiyum perumiyum oruṅku uṭiyaḷākaliṉ talimakaṉi neruṅku taṟku añcuvaḷ. āyiṉum  tōḻi yeṉṟa muṟiyil piṟaroṭu naṉku uriyāṭi aṟivaṉa aṟintu āvaṉa ceytaṟkaṇ avaṭku urimi perituṇṭu. ataṉāl avaḷ veḷiyē taṉittu iyaṅkutal iyalpāyiṟṟu. ākavē, talimakaṉ tōḻiyik kaṇṭu avaḷatu naṭpiṉip peṟal  vēṇṭi oru kuṟiyuṭiyāṉ pōl neruṅka vēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. talimakaḷum tōḻiyum  ēṉi āyamakaḷirum viḷiyāṭiyum nīrāṭiyum pūkkoytum  iṉpuṟum iṭaṅkaṭkut talimakaṉum ceṉṟu avarkaṭku vēṇṭuvaṉa utavit tōḻiyōṭu uriyāṭaṟku iṭam peṟṟu avaḷpāl ōr kuṟiyuṭiyāṉ pōl irantu piṉṉiṉṟu oḻukuvaṉ. nāḷaṭivil tōḻi talimakaḷ kuṟippi aṟintu koḷvāḷ. tōḻi vāyilākat talimakaḷip peṟak karutu taliṉ  taṉakkum talimakaṭkum muṉpē kātaluṟavuṇṭeṉpati  avaṉ vāyviṭṭu uriyāṉ. talimakaḷum taṉ toṭarpit tōḻiyaṟiyap pulappaṭuttāmiyiṉ, tōḻi taliviyiṉ arumiyum perumiyum nōkkit talimakaṉ kuṟippi  urippa teṉpatu peṇmikkum ulakiyalukkum ovvāmi  uṇarkiṉṟāḷ. ataṉāl, tōḻi talimakaṉic cēṭpaṭuk kiṉṟāḷ. taṉ muyaṟci payaṉpaṭāmi kāṇum talimakaṉ āṟṟāmi mītūrat talimakaḷukkum taṉakkum uḷatāya uṟaviṉit tōḻiyuṇarap piyak kuṟikkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉuṭiya oḻukkamum uriyum kuṟippum kaṇṭa tōḻi iruvarkkum muṉpē uṟavuṇṭeṉa uṇarkiṉṟāḷ; āyiṉum atuvē paṟṟukkōṭāka avaḷ talimakaḷpāl talivaṉ muyaṟciyiyum oḻukalāṟṟiyum urittu avaḷ  kuṟippi ārāya muṟpaṭuvatu aṟamākāmiyiṉ, avaḷ cēṭpaṭiyiṉiyē toṭarntu ceykiṉṟāḷ. “kātaliyē, nī ceṉṟu kiḷikaṭiyum tiṉip puṉattukku ñāyiṟu tōṉṟi viḷaṅkum kālip pōtil varavō? aṉṟi, nām mutaṟkaṇ talikkūṭiya maliccāralil viḷiyāṭumiṭattu varavō? eṉ neñcu koḷḷumāṟu iṉ colloṉṟu vaḻaṅkuka. yāṉ niṉ vāleyiṟu ūṟiya nīri yuṇṭumakiḻvēṉ eṉa yāṉ moḻintēṉāka, eṉmoḻik kiraṅkip pōntu taṉakku aminta kuṟiyiṭattē koṇṭu ceṉṟu taṉ iṉmoḻiyāl eṉṉi iṉpuṟutti ēṟṟiṉip pirintēkum piṇimāṉpōla eṉṉi aṉpu kaṉiya nōkkik koṇṭu tāṉ uṟiyum ciṟukuṭikku nī ceṉṟaṉi. ceṉṟa niṉ celpuṟam nōkki niṉṉic cella viṭuttēṉ;aṅṅaṉam viṭuttaṟkut tuṇiceyta eṉ neñcam niṉṉi niṉittali viṭātākiṉṟatu, eṉ ceyvēṉ?’’ eṉṟu iyampiṉāṉ.
talimakaṉuṭiya immoḻiyiṉkaṇ, talimakaṭkum taṉakkum taṉitta niliyil tōṉṟiya kātaluṟavit tōḻik kurittal vēṇṭum eṉpatu karuttāyiṉum, taṉimiyil tōṉṟi uḷḷattē oṭuṅki niṟṟaṟkuriyatākiya muṉṉi yuṟavip piṟar cevippaṭumaḷavil vāyviṭṭurittaṟku āṟṟāmi avaṉpāl nilavuvatip pulappaṭuttak kaṇṭa tōḻi virintu matiyuṭampaṭumāṟu ceyyum ceyaṟciṟappu nōkkiya am maḷḷaṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ataṉit toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
taḷircēr taṇṭaḻi tii nunti
1kuḷirkoḷ viyaṉpuṉat teṟpaṭa varukō
kuṟuñcuṉik kuvaḷi aṭiccinām puṇariya
naṟuntaṇ cāral āṭukam varukō
iṉcol 2mēvaliṉ iyalumeṉ neñcuṇak
kūṟiṉi maṭantiniṉ kūreyi  ṟuṇkeṉa
yāṉṟaṉ moḻitaliṉ moḻiyetir vantu 
tāṉcey 3kuṟivayiṉ iṉiya kūṟi
ēṟupiri maṭappiṇi kaṭuppa vēṟupaṭ 
ṭuṟukaḻi nivappiṉ ciṟukuṭip peyarum
koṭicci 1celpuṟam nōkki
2viṭutta neñcam viṭalol lātē.
itu piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṉ āṟṟāṉāyt tōḻi kēṭpat taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu. 
uri: 
taḷircēr taṇtaḻi tii - taḷirkaḷiyum malar kaḷiyum koṇṭu toṭukkappaṭṭa taṇṇiya taḻiyuṭiyi yuṭuttu; nunti kuḷirkoḷ viyaṉpuṉattu eṟpaṭa varukō - niṉ tantiyiṉuṭiya kuḷireṉṉum karuvi koṇṭu kiḷi kaṭintu nī kāvalpuriyum akaṉṟa tiṉippuṉattukku ñāyiṟu tōṉṟi viḷaṅkum kālippōtil varavō; kuṟuñcuṉik kuvaḷi aṭicci - kuṟukiya cuṉiyiṭattu malarnta kuvaḷip pūvi aṇintu; nām puṇariya naṟuntaṇcāral āṭukam  varukō- nām mutaṟkaṇ talippeytu kūṭiya naṟiya taṇṇiya maliccāralil viḷiyāṭutaṟku varavō; iṉcol mēvaliṉ iyalum eṉ neñcuṇa - niṉatu iṉiya colli virumpiyoḻukum eṉ neñcu koḷḷumāṟu; maṭanti -maṭantiyē; iṉi kūṟu - ippoḻutu kūṟuka; niṉ kūr eyiṟu uṇku eṉa - niṉ kūriya paṟkaḷiṭattu ūṟum amutiṉip parukuvēṉ eṉṟu; yāṉ taṉ moḻitaliṉ - yāṉ taṉakku urittalāl; moḻi etirvantu - ataṟku viṭikūṟuvāḷ pōla etirēvantu; tāṉ cey kuṟivayiṉ iṉiya kūṟi - tāṉ karutiya kuṟiyiṭattē eṉṉik koṇṭu ceṉṟu iṉiya coṟkaḷic colli; ēṟupiri maṭappiṇi kaṭuppa - āṇākiya kalimāṉip pirintēkum piṇi māṉip pōla; vēṟupaṭṭu - vēṟāka  nīṅki; uṟukaḻinivappiṉ ciṟukuṭip peyarum -mikka  mūṅkilkaḷ uyarnta ciṟukuṭi nōkkic cellum; koṭicci celpuṟam nōkki - koṭicci yākiya talimakaḷuṭiya mutukiṉi nōkki; viṭutta neñcam- viṭi vaḻaṅkiya eṉ neñcam; viṭal ollātu - niṉit talik kiviṭātu ākaliṉ yāṉ eṉceyvēṉ? e.ṟu. 
maṭanti, puṉattu eṟpaṭa varukō, cāral āṭukam varukō, iṉcol mēvaliṉ neñcuṇa iṉik kūṟu. niṉ eyiṟu uṇku eṉa yāṉ moḻitaliṉ, moḻiyetir vantu kuṟivayiṉ iṉiya kūṟi, vēṟupaṭṭuc ciṟukuṭip peyarum koṭicci celpuṟam nōkki viṭutta neñcam  viṭalollātu. eṉceyvēṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. palvaki malarkaḷum viravit toṭukkappaṭu māyiṉum taḻi yeṉappaṭuvataṟkuc ciṟantami yiṉ taḷircēr taṇṭaḻi eṉṟār. taḻi toṭuttu aṇintu koṇṭu tiṉippuṉattēyiruntu kiḷikaṭitaliṉ, taṇṭaḻi tii eṉṟār. taḻi tii  varukō eṉa iyittu taḻi toṭuttuk koṇṭu varukō eṉa urippiṉu mām. kuḷir, kiḷikaṭiyum oruvakik karuvi. kuḷirum taṭṭiyum kavaṇum koṇṭu puṉaṅkāppa vākaliṉ, kuḷirkoḷ viyaṉpuṉam eṉappaṭṭatu; iṉi, kuḷircci koṇṭa puṉameṉṟumām. eṟpaṭa eṉṟaviṭattup paṭutal, tōṉṟutal; taḻiyuṭi aṇitaṟkum, taḻikoṇṭu varutaṟkum ñāyiṟu maṟinta iruḷmālippōtu poruntāmiyiṉ, ivvāṟu urikkap paṭṭatu; ammālip pōtu puṉaṅkāvaṟkum iyiyāmi aṟika. aṭiccutal, cūṭutal. mēval, virumputal. neñcuṇak kūṟal, aiyan tiripiṉṟit teḷiya uṇarumāṟu kūṟutal; “eñcā vañciṉam neñcuṇak kūṟi’’1  eṉap piṟarum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. eyiṟu uṇṭal, mukki2 koṭuttal. kuṟiyiṭam talimakaḷāl kuṟippiṭum iyalpiṟṟākaliṉ, kāṇcey kuṟiyiṭam eṉṟār. “kaḷamcuṭṭu kiḷavi kiḻaviya tākum”3 eṉpa. piṇi yeṉṟami yiṉ ēṟu, ataṉ ā'82kiya kaliyiṉ mēṟṟu. mūṅkiṟ putarkaḷ uyarntu vēlipōl niṟkum ciṟukuṭi eṉṟaṟku uṟukaḻi nivappiṉ ciṟukuṭi eṉṟār. ciṟukuṭi kuṟiñci nilattu  ūr. 
iyaṟkippuṇarccikkaṇ talimakaḷ tiṉippuṉattē taḻiyuṭi toṭuttaṇintu puṉaṅkāval purintoḻukiṉā ḷākaliṉ, ataṉic ciṟappittut taḷircēr taṇṭaḻi tii eṉṟum, kuḷirkoḷ viyaṉpuṉam eṉṟum, iraṇṭāṅ kūṭṭattum mūṉṟāṅ kūṭṭattum cuṉippūkkuṟṟu māli toṭuttu maliccāralil viḷiyāṭi makiḻntamiyiṉ ataṉi vitantu kuṟuñcuṉik kuvaḷi aṭicci eṉṟum, naṟuntaṇ cāral eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. iyalpākavē iṉmoḻi vaḻaṅkum tiṟattiṉa ḷākiya talimakaḷ, nāṇamikutiyāl mikac cilavāya coṟkaḷi yuṭiyaḷāyiṉamiyiṉ moḻikēṭkum vēṭki mikku niṉṟami viḷaṅka, iṉcol mēvaliṉ iyalum eṉ neñcum eṉṟum, irupāṟpaṭṭa taṉ viṉāvukku yātu viṭi kūṟuvaḷō eṉat taṉ neñcu aiyattāl alamantamiyiṉ, neñcuṇa iṉik kūṟu eṉṟum, putitu tōṉṟiya kāmakkiḷarcci yāl mayaṅkiya nōkkamum ceyalum uṭiyaḷāṉami paṟṟi maṭanti eṉṟum, taṉatu vēṭki mikuti tōṉṟa, niṉ kūreyiṟu uṇku eṉṟum urittāṉ. iṅṅaṉam ārāmiyāl pala coṟkaḷi yuritta talimakaṉukku viṭikūṟum vakiyil nāṇamikutiyāl nāveḻāmiyiṉ taṉ kaṭpārviyāl kuṟiyiṭam kāṭṭic ceṉṟaṉaḷ eṉṟaṟku, moḻi yetirvantu tāṉcey kuṟivayiṉ iṉiya kūṟi yeṉṟum. ēṟupiri maṭappiṇi kaṭuppac ceṉṟaṉaḷ eṉṟum tōḻikku muṉṉuṟa vuṇarttiya vāṟu kāṇka. puṇarccikkaṇ kaṇṭa meṉmi piriviṉkaṇ māṟi vaṉmiyuṟṟut tōṉṟiṉamipaṟṟi vēṟupaṭṭu eṉṟum, celpuṟam nōkki viṭutta eṉ neñcam, moḻiyetirvantu niṉṟa iṉmukam nōkkip peṟṟa kūṭṭatti maṟavātāyiṟ ṟeṉpāṉ, viṭutta neñcam viṭal ollātu eṉṟum urit tāṉ. itaṉik kēṭṭa tōḻi, piṟaḷākiya eṉ cevippaṭak kūṟaluṟṟavaṉ, iṉiyum cēṭpaṭukkiṉ, paḻiyām eṉpatuṇarntu avaṉi aruḷi nōkkuvatu payaṉām eṉa vuṇarka. 
“meytoṭṭup payiṟal”1 eṉṉum nūṟpāvil, “colla vaṭ cārttaliṟ pulliya vakiyiṉum” eṉṟaviṭattu. “vaki eṉṟataṉāṉē, itaṉiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavum koḷka’’ eṉṟu urittu ippāṭṭik kāṭṭuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.

205. iḷanākaṉār 
maṉiyaṟam purintuvarum talimakaṉ kaṭamiyāya viṉi yoṉṟu kuṟittut taṉmaṉiviyiṉ nīṅkic cellavēṇṭiya vaṉāṉāṉ. putu maṇam puṇarntoḻukum putuvāḻvil iv viṉivayiṟ pirivu tōṉṟiyatum, maṉivipāṟ koṇṭuḷḷa kātaluṇarvu avaṉatu iḷamiyuḷḷattit taṉvari niṟuttavē, pirivāluḷavākiya tuṉpamum taliviyatu āṟṟāmiyum, atu vaḻiyāka avaḷ mēṉivēṟupaṭiṉ tōṉṟum kaviṉkēṭum avaṉip peritum varuttiṉa. kaṭami nekiḻiṉ varum kuṟṟamum avaṉmaṉakkaṇṇil tōṉṟiṟṟu. kaṭamikkum kātalukkum pōr uṇṭāyiṟṟu. pōr nikaḻiṭamākiya avaṉatu uḷḷam peritum kuḻampiṟṟu. anniliyil avaṉ, taṉṉik kaṭami vaḻic cellumāṟu kaṭṭurikkum neñciṉi nōkki, “nī āṟṟakkarutum viṉinikaḻccikkuk kaṭattaṟkariya periya kāṭṭi ṉūṭē cellavēṇṭum; viṉiyē āṇmikku uyirp pātaliṉ, ataṉi yeṇṇumiṭattu maṉivipāl uḷatākum kātal peritaṉṟu eṉpāyāyiṉ, piriviṉkaṇ kātaluṇarvu viḷivikkum varuttamikutiyāl talivi mēṉiyil viḷaṅkum māmik kaviṉ keṭṭateṉṟē kōṭal vēṇṭum’’ eṉṟu varuntik kūṟiṉāṉ. 
avaṉ kūṟṟiṉkaṇ, viṉiyāṇmiyip paḻikkātu. iṭiyil kaṭattaṟkuriya kāṉattiṉ arumiyum, taliviyatu māmikkaviṉ kēṭum eṭuttōtik, kātaluṇarviṉum kaṭami yuṇarvu ciṟantu niṟkum tiṟam kuṟippāyp pulappaṭutal kaṇṭa iḷanākaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭu kiṉṟār. iḷanākaṉāratu pulamiyuḷḷattu iḷaminalam kātaliṉum kaṭamikkaṇ ciṟantu niṟpatu itaṉāl nām uṇarak kiṭappatu kāṇka. 
aruvi yārkkum peruvari yaṭukkat 
tāḷi naṉmāṉ 2vēṭṭeḻupu kōḷukirp
pūmpoṟi uḻuvi tolicciya vinnuti
ēntuveṇ kōṭṭu vayakkaḷi ṟīrkkum 
tuṉṉaruṅ kāṉam eṉṉāy nīyē 
kuvaḷi yuṇkaṇ ivaḷīṇ ṭoḻiya
āḷviṉik kakaṟi yāyiṉ 3niṉṉoṭu
pōyiṉṟu kollō tāṉē paṭappik
koṭumuḷ īṅki 1neṭumā antaḷir
nīrmali kataḻpeyal taliiya 
āyniṟam puriyumivaḷ māmik kaviṉē.
itu talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc collic celavaḻuṅkiyatu; tōḻi celavaḻuṅkac colliyatūumām. 
uri: 
aruvi ārkkum peruvari aṭukkattu - aruvikaḷ muḻaṅkum periyamalippakkattē; āḷi naṉmāṉ vēṭṭu eḻupu - āḷiyākiya nalla vilaṅku irikuṟittu vēṭṭam ceṉṟu; kōḷ ukirp pūmpoṟi uḻuvi tolicciya - koḷḷutal valla ukiriyum aḻakiya poṟikaḷiyu muṭiya puliyoṭu porutu ataṉik koṉṟa; vinnuti ēntu veṇkōṭṭu vayakkaḷiṟu - mikavum kūriya uyarnta veṇmiyāṉa kompukaḷiyuṭiya veṟṟi mikka kaḷiṟ ṟiṉik koṉṟu; īrkkum - taṉ muḻiñciṟku īrttuk koṇṭu cellum; tuṉṉarum kāṉam eṉṉāy - pukutaṟ kariya kāṭu eṉpatu karutāmal; kuvaḷi uṇ kaṇ avaḷ īṇṭu oḻiya - kuvaḷi malar pōlum uṇkaṇkaḷiyuṭiya ivaḷ iṅkē taṉimiyuṟṟirukka; nī āḷviṉikku akaṟi - nī āḷviṉi karutip pirintu cellukiṉṟāy; āyiṉ -ārā yiṉ; niṉṉoṭu pōyiṉṟu - niṉṉōṭē uṭaṉceṉṟa tām; paṭappik koṭumuḷ īṅki neṭumā antaḷir - paṭappiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa vaḷinta muḷḷiyuṭiya īṅkiyiṉ neṭiya aḻakiya taḷir; nīr mali kataḻ peyal taliiya - nīrmikku virintu peyyum maḻiyāl naṉintavaḻip piṟakkum; āyniṟam puriyum ivaḷ māmikkaviṉ - aḻakiya taḷiriṉ niṟam pōlum ivaḷuṭiya māmikkaviṉ, e-ṟu. 
nī tuṉṉarum kāṉam eṉṉāy; akaṟi; āyiṉ, niṉṉoṭu ivaḷ māmikkaviṉ pōyiṉṟu eṉakkūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. āḷinaṉmāṉ, arimāvakiyuḷ oṉṟu. uḻuvi,puli. vinnuti ēntuveṇ kōṭṭu vayakkaḷiṟu eṉṟataṉāl, kaḷiṟu uḻuviyi veṉṟami peṟṟām. tolicciya, koṉṟa . eṉṉāy: muṟṟeccam. uṇkaṇ, mi tīṭṭiya kaṇ. kollum ōvum acinili. tāṉē, kaṭṭuric cuvipaṭa vantatu. īṅki muḷḷuṭiya teṉpatu, “muṭkompīṅki”1 eṉavum “koṭu muḷ īṅki”2 eṉavum, nīrāl naṉinta ataṉ taḷir makaḷir mēṉikku uvamiyātali, “māri īṅki māttaḷi raṉṉa ammā mēṉi yāyiḻi makaḷir”3 eṉavum cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka āyniṟam, aḻakiya niṟam. māmikkaviṉ. māntaḷiriṉ taṉmiyi yuṭiya niṟavaḻaku. oḷiyuṭimi pulappaṭutaṟku nīril naṉital vēṇṭappaṭṭatu. 
kūrukirum vāleyiṟumuṭiya pūmpoṟi yuḻuviyik kaḷiṟu taṉ vinnuti veṇkōṭṭāṟ koṉṟami kūṟiṉāṉ, āḷi naṉmāṉiṉ āṟṟal tōṟṟuvittaṟku. ataṉāṟ payaṉ, taṉatu maṉattiṇmiyi veṉṟu taṉvari niṟuttip paṇi koṇṭa taliviyatu māmik kaviṉiṉ māṇpu ciṟappit turittavāṟām. attakiya kaḷiṟāyiṉum aḵtu āḷi naṉ māṉāl  kollappaṭṭami kūṟiyataṉāl. taliviyatu māmik kaviṉ taṉ piriviṭattuḷatākum pacaliyāl uṇṇappaṭ ṭoḻiyum eṉṟum kūṟiyavāṟu. ivvāṟu talimakaḷkaviṉ pacaliyāl keṭuvatu iṟicciyāl cuṭṭappaṭṭamiyiṉ, veḷippaṭiyāka ivaḷ māmikkaviṉ niṉṉoṭu pōyiṉṟu kol eṉṟu taṉ tuṇivum virivum tōṉṟa iṟantakāla vāypāṭṭāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ. cellutaṟkuriya kāṉattiṉ koṭumi yuṇarntum  añcātu celvatu karutiyatu paṟṟi neñciṉi viyantu tuṉṉaruṅ kāṉam eṉṉāy eṉṟum, eṉiṉum vāḻkkikkut tuṇiyākak koṇṭa kātaliyi niṉiyātu āḷviṇiyit tuṇiyākak koṇṭatu kuṟṟameṉpāṉ, kuvaḷiyuṇkaṇ ivaḷ īṇṭoḻiya eṉṟum, āḷviṉikku akaṟi eṉṟum, iṅṅaṉam muṉṉukkuppiṉ mura'82ṉa ceyal niṉpāl nikaḻtal ārāytaṟkuriya tāyiṉamiyiṉ āyiṉ eṉṟum,  ārāynta vaḻi ivaḷatu māmikkaviṉ niṉṉōṭē kaḻiyu meṉpatu teḷiyappaṭṭa teṉpāṉ, niṉṉoṭu pōyiṉṟu kollō ivaḷ māmikkaviṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ, ivaḷi vāḻkkit tuṇiyākak kōṭaṟku mutalāy niṉṉi ivaḷ pāṟ oṉṟuvittup piṇittatu immāmik kaviṉ eṉpāṉ ataṉiyē vitantu ivaḷ māmikkaviṉ eṉṟu eṭut tōtiṉāṉ. “ākkam  karuti mutaliḻakkum  ceyviṉi’’1 ūkkap paṭum  takuti yuṭittaṉṟu eṉpatu. māmik kēṭu kuṟittu neñcu celavaḻuṅkiṉum, celaviṉkaṇ tōṉṟum pacali talimakaṉ puṇarap piritalum piriyap puṇarttalumākiya iyalpiṟṟāy, āḷviṉikkēṭu pōla māṟāppaḻiyum vaciyum payappatiṉmi yiṉ, talivaṉ ataṉi niṉintu piritaliyē ceyvāṉ eṉṟu aṟika. “celaviṭiyaḻuṅkal cellāmi yaṉṟē, vaṉpuṟi kuṟittal tavircci  yākum”2 eṉa āciriyar kūṟutal kāṇka. 
“karaṇatti ṉamintu muṭintakāli”3 eṉṟa nūṟpāviṉkaṇ, “vēṟṟunāṭ ṭakalvayiṉ viḻumat tāṉum” eṉpataṉ uriyil “viḻumamāvaṉa piriyak karutiyavaṉ paḷḷiyiṭattuk kaṉaviṟ kūṟuvaṉavum, pōvōmō tavirvēmō eṉa varuntik kūṟu vaṉavum, ivaḷ nalaṉ tiriyumeṉṟalum, piriyuṅkol eṉṟu aiyuṟṟa taliviyi aiyantīrak kūṟalum, neñciṟkuc colli aḻuṅkutalum piṟavumām” eṉṟu kūṟi ippāṭṭik kāṭṭi, “iḵtu ivaḷ nalaṉaḻiyu meṉṟu celavaḻuṅkiyatu” eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
206. aiyūr muṭavaṉār
ivar peyar aiyūr mūvaṉār eṉṟum puṟappāṭṭu ēṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. muṭavaṉ eṉṟa peyari nōkka, mūvaṉ eṉṟa peyarē uṇmiyākalāmeṉa niṉittaṟku iṭamuṇṭu. āyiṉum muṭavaṉār eṉṟa peyarē ēṭukaḷ palavaṟṟilum ēṉittoki nūlkaḷilum  kāṇappaṭuvatu paṟṟi atuvē īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. aiyūr eṉṟa peyaruṭiya vūrkaḷ tamiḻakattil palavuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ pāṇṭi nāṭṭil aiyū reṉṟum, ciṟṟiyūr eṉṟum pēriyūr eṉṟum pala ūrkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāṇṭiyaṉ kūṭakārattut tuñciya māṟaṉ vaḻuti yeṉpāṉiyum, cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉiyum ivar pāṭiyaṉa eṉac cila pāṭṭukkaḷ puṟanāṉūṟṟuḷ kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaruḷ māṟaṉ vaḻutiyiṉ ciṟappi nēriṟkaṇṭu “nīrmikiṟ ciṟiyu milli tīmikiṉ maṉṉuyir niḻaṟṟum niḻalu milli, vaḷimikiṉ valiyu milli oḷimikku, avaṟṟōraṉṉa ciṉappōr vaḻuti eṉṟu pārāṭṭi yuḷḷār; kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ iṟanta piṉṉum iruntavarākaliṉ, avaṉ iṟappiṉkaṇ ivar kiyaṟṟup pāṭumiṭattu, “pulavar pukaḻnta poyyā  nallici, virikatir ñāyiṟu vicumpivarn taṉṉa, cēṇviḷaṅku ciṟappiṉ cempiyar marukaṉ, koṭi nuṭaṅkuyāṉi neṭumā vaḷavaṉ, tēvarulaka meytiṉaṉ” eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟār . iṉi maturi māvaṭṭattut tiṇṭukkallukku mēṟkil uḷḷa malit toṭaril viḷaṅkum tōṉṟikkuṭiyil paṇṭināḷil vāḻnta tāmaṉ eṉṟa celvapperumakaṉi ivar pāṭiyirukkiṉṟār; avvūrkkut teṉpakutiyil nilak kōṭṭi vaṭṭattil aiyūr eṉṟa paḻamiyāṉa ūroṉṟum uḷatu. tōṉṟikkōṉ iccāṉṟōratu nallicip pulaminalam kaṇṭu ceyta vaḷḷaṉmiyi viyantu ivar avaṉip pāṭiya pāṭṭu avaṉi, “aṟavar aṟavaṉ maṟavar maṟavaṉ, maḷḷar maḷḷaṉ tollōr marukaṉ” eṉṟum kuṟikkiṟatu. avaṉi avar tām ūrntu cellum vaṇṭiyi īrttaṟkeṉa orupakaṭu vēṇṭiṉarāka, avaṉ “vicumpiṉ mīṉpūt taṉṉa uruvap paṉṉiri, ūrtiyoṭu nalkiṉāṉ” eṉṟu parivuṭaṉ pāṭu kiṉṟār. ivaṟṟi nōkka, ivaratu aiyūr nilakkōṭṭi vaṭṭattu aiyūr eṉṟu kōṭal valiyuṭiyatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. cellikkōmāṉ ātaṉ eḻiṉi periya kaḷiṟoṉṟiṉ neñcil neṭuvēl pāycci veṉṟavaralāṟoṉṟiyum ivar tām pāṭiya akappāṭṭoṉṟil kuṟikkiṉṟār. ivaruṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ ēṉip puṟanāṉūṟu kuṟuntoki eṉṟa toki nūlkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kātalāṟ piṇippuṇṭu kaḷaviṉkaṇ kuṟiyiṭattē iraviṉum pakaliṉum talippeytu iṉpuṟṟu varum talimakkaḷuḷ, talivipāl kātal ciṟantu niṟṟalik kaṇṭatōḻi, tali makaṉukkuk kaḷaviṉkaṇ uḷavākum iṭiyūṟukaḷik kuṟippāka  uṇarttit taliviyi varintu koḷḷumāṟu teruṭṭum karuttuṭiyaḷāṉāḷ. talimakaṉō akkātal piṉṉum peruki uyirppiṇip pākumaḷavu vaḷartal vēṇṭumeṉṟa eṇṇattāl kaḷaviṉiyē virumpi yoḻukiṉāṉ. orunāḷ talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉāka, ataṉi uṇarnta tōḻi, taliviyoṭu uriyāṭuvāḷ pōṉṟu, talimakaṉ cevippaṭum vakiyil cila kūṟalāṉāḷ: “tōḻi, nampuṉattē niṟkum tiṉik katirkaḷ viḷintu pālkoṇṭu talicāyat  toṭaṅkiṉa eṉṟum, iṉi avaṟṟiṟ kiḷiyiṉam paṭintu katirkavarumeṉṟum, avviṭattē taṭṭiyum kavaṇum  koṇṭu ceṉṟu kaṭital vēṇṭum eṉṟum nerunal tanti pōntu tāyiṭattē urittāṉ; atukēṭṭu, naṉṉāḷ kāṭṭum vēṅkiyum iṉimalarvatām eṉṟu colli, yāy āṅkēniṉṟa eṉmukatti yātu karutiyō nōkkiṉāḷ; ataṉāl, avaḷ nīvir tiṉippuṉam celka eṉpāḷō? cellātu illiṉ kaṇṇē ceṟippuṇka eṉ pāḷō? talimakaṟkum namakkumuḷḷa kēṇmiyi aṟivāḷō? avaḷ karutiyatu yātō?terintilēṉ’’ eṉṟu  kūṟiṉāḷ.
tōḻiyiṉ ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, taṅkaḷ kaḷaviṉit tāyaṟin taṉaḷ eṉpatum, ataṉāl iṟceṟippum, varitaṟku muyaliṉ aḵtu  eḷitiṟ kikūṭuntiṟaṉum talimakaṟ kuṇartti varivu kaṭāvum kuṟippu aḻakuṟa amintiruttal  kaṇṭa aiyūrmuṭa
vaṉār ippāṭṭiṉip pāṭiyuḷḷār. 
tuyttalip puṉiṟṟukkural 1pālkoḷ piṟiñcit
2tōṭutalip pirintaṉa ēṉal eṉṟum 
tuṟukal mīmicik kuṟuvaṉa kuḻīic
cevvāyp pāciṉam 3kavarumeṉ ṟavvāyt 
taṭṭiyum 4puṭikka kavaṇum toṭukkeṉa 
entivan turittaṉa ṉāka  aṉṉiyum 
naṉṉāḷ vēṅkiyum alarkamā iṉiyeṉa
eṉmuka nōkkiṉaḷ evaṉkol tōḻi
5ceṉṟī keṉṉuṅkol  6ceṟippal eṉṉuṅkol 
kalkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṇmi 
7aṟiyunaḷ kollaḵ taṟikaleṉ yāṉē.
itu talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi colliyatu
uri: 
tuyttalip puṉiṟṟukkural - taliyil tuyyiṉi yuṭiya mikka iḷaṅkatirkaḷ; pāl koḷpu iṟiñcit tōṭutalip pirintaṉa ēṉal eṉṟum - pālkoṇṭu muṟṟit talicāyntu mūṭiyirunta tōṭukaḷ nīṅkat talippaṭṭaṉa tiṉippayirkaḷ eṉṟum; tuṟukal mīmicik kuṟuvaṉa kuḻīi - tuṟukaṟ pāṟikaḷiṉmēl tiṉik katirkaḷik koytaṟ poruṭṭuk kūṭiyiruntu; cevvāyp pāciṉam kavarum eṉṟum - civanta vāyiyuṭiya paciya kiḷḷiyiṉam tiṉik katirkaḷik kavarntu koṇṭu pōkum eṉṟum; avvāy taṭṭiyum puṭikka kavaṇiyum toṭukka eṉa- appuṉat tiṭam ceṉṟu taṭṭiyip puṭittum kavaṇiyi eṟintum kiḷikaḷik kaṭital vēṇṭum eṉṟum; enti vantu urit taṉaṉāka - em  tanti pōntu tāypāl urittāṉāka atu kēṭṭu; aṉṉiyum - nam tāyum; iṉinaṉṉāḷ vēṅkiyum alarkamā eṉa eṉmuka nōkkiṉaḷ - iṉit tirumaṇattuk kuriya naṉṉāḷik kāṭṭum vēṅkiyum malarvatām  eṉṟu colli eṉ mukatti uṟṟunōkkiṉāḷ; tōḻi evaṉ kol - tōḻi, yātu karutiyō; ceṉṟīka eṉṉuṅkol - tiṉippuṉam ceṉṟu varuka eṉpāḷō; ceṟippal eṉṉuṅ kolpuṉam cellaviṭutal naṉṟaṉṟu maṉikkaṇṇē ceṟippuṇka eṉṟu colluvāḷō; kalkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṇmi aṟiyunaḷ kol - malināṭaṉatu naṭpiṉi aṟintu koṇṭāḷō; yāṉ aḵtu aṟikaleṉ - yāṉ avaḷ aṟintatiṟam aṟiyēṉ, kāṇ e.ṟu.
ēṉal kural pāl koḷpu iṟiñcit tōṭu talik koṇṭaṉa eṉṟum, pāciṉam kuṟuvaṉa kuḻīik kavarum eṉṟum, avvāyt taṭṭiyum puṭikka, kavaṇiyum toṭukka eṉavum enti vantu urittaṉaṉāka, tōḻi, aṉṉiyum, iṉi vēṅkiyum alarkamā eṉa urittu eṉmukam nōkkiṉāḷ; evaṉkolō; ceṉṟīkeṉṉuṅ kol, ceṟippal eṉṉuṅ kol, nāṭaṉkēṇmi  aṟiyunaḷkol, aḵtu yāṉ aṟikaleṉ kāṇ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. tiṉikkatiril tiṉi maṇikaṭku iṭiyē kūriya mayirkkāmpu pōl niṟpaṉavē īṇṭut tuyyeṉap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. tōṭumūṭappeṟṟut tōṉṟum katir muṟṟat toṭaṅkiyatum mella mellat tōṭu virintu nīṅkiya cevviyil puṉiṟṟukkural eṉappaṭum. puṉiṟu, īṉṟa aṇimiyiluḷḷa cevvi. naṉku muṟṟumiṭattut tiṉiyiṉkaṇ pāl kāṇappaṭutaliṉ pālkoḷpu eṉṟum, muṟṟiya tiṉi maṇikaḷit tāṅkamāṭṭāta melliya kompātaliṉ atu talicāyntuviṭutal paṟṟi iṟiñci yeṉṟum, muṟṟiya katir cevvē viḷitaṟku veyilum kāṟṟum niṟiya vēṇṭiyirut taliṉ tōṭukaḷ ularntu utirntu pōvataṉāl tōṭutalip pirintaṉa ēṉal eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. mali kuṉṟukaḷiṉ pakkattē tōṉṟum kariya periya taṉittaṉip pāṟikaḷit tuṟukal eṉpar. kuṟutal, koytal, kiḷiyip pāciṉam eṉpatu vaḻakku; “vaḷivāyp pāciṉam”1 eṉpatu kāṇka, taṭṭi, nilattil taṭṭiyavaḻi mikka ōciyeṭukkum oruvakik  kiḷikaṭikaruvi kavaṇi, kavaṇ. vēṅki malaruṅ kālattil makkaḷ tirumaṇaṅ kaḷi nikaḻttuvatu paṟṟi naṉṉāḷ vēṅki eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. mā: viyaṅkōḷ acinili. ceṉṟīka:  muṟṟuviṉit tiri col, kol : aiyaviṭiccol.
ciṟippuṟamāka vantu niṉṟa talimakaṉik kaṇṭa tōḻi talimakaḷi avviṭattukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu avaḷoṭu uriyāṭupavaḷ pōlat talimakaṉ cevippaṭumāṟu, “tōḻi, tiṉippuṉam ceṉṟu pōnta tanti tiṉiviḷivu kūṟi ataṉik koytaḻikkum vakiyil kiḷḷiyiṉam kuḻumu kiṉṟaṉa eṉpāṉāy, kural pālkoḷpu iṟiñcit tōṭutalip pirintaṉa ēṉal eṉṟum, cevvāyp pāciṉam kuḻīik kavarum eṉṟum, ataṉāl nām ceyya vēṇṭuvaṉa  ivi yeṉpāṉ taṭṭiyum puṭikka kavaṇiyum toṭukka eṉa enti vantu urittaṉaṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. puṉaṅkāvaṟ pōtaṉṟi vēṟu kālaṅkaḷil vēṇṭappaṭātu maṉiyiṉkaṇ oṭukkappaṭutaliṉ, taṭṭi kavaṇ mutaliya kiḷikaṭi karuvikaḷic cemmi ceytuvittal vēṇṭit taṭṭiyum puṭikka, kavaṇiyum toṭukka eṉat tāykku urittāṉ eṉka. tiṉiyaṟuttapiṉ puṉavarkku vēṇṭiya viḷi poruḷum puṉattē vēṟu toḻiliṉmiyum uḷavā taliṉ, avarkaḷ tirumaṇamutaliya ciṟappukkaḷil karuttic celuttupa vātalāl, maṇattukkuriya cevvi eytiya makaṭkut tirumaṇam ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟa kuṟippit tantikkuk kuṟippāḷāy aṉṉiyum naṉṉāḷ vēṅkiyum alarkamā iṉi eṉṟāḷ. maṇam ceytu kōṭaṟkuriyavaḷ makaḷātaliṉ avaḷiyum ataṟku matiyuṭampaṭuttal vēṇṭi, vēṅki naṉṉāḷ alarka eṉṟamiyiṉ, avaḷ kuṟippaṟivāḷāyt tōḻiyi nōkkiṉāḷ, tōḻiyoṭaṉṟi makaḷoṭu ataṉi nērē yuriyāḷākaliṉ. āyiṉum, tōḻi, talimakaṟku av vuriyāṭṭiṉi aṟivikkumāṟṟal taliviyoṭu kūṟṟu nikaḻttutalāl eṉmukam nōkkiṉaḷ eṉṟum, eṉṉinōkku taliṉ nam kaḷavoḻukkatti nam tāy aṟivaḷ pōlum eṉṟu aiyuṟṟami tōṉṟa, evaṉ kol tōḻi eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. tantiyoṭu uriyāṭiyatu tiṉippuṉattukkuc cellaviṭu vaḷeṉṟa kuṟippum, eṉmukam nōkkiyatu cellaviṭātu illiṭattē ceṟippaḷ eṉṟa kuṟippum tōṉṟa niṟṟaliṉ, ceṉṟīka eṉṉuṅkol ceṟippal eṉṉuṅ kol eṉṟum, iṟceṟittaṟkuk kāraṇam talimakaṉatu naṭpiṉi avaḷ aṟintāḷātal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟkuk kalkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṇmi aṟintāḷātal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟkuk kalkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṇmi aṟiyunaḷkol eṉṟum, ataṉi avaḷ aṟintavāṟu taṉakkut teriyātu eṉṟaṟku aḵtu aṟikaleṉ yāṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. itu kēṭṭut talivaṉ teruṇṭu varivāṉāvatu payaṉ.
207. āvūr kiḻār makaṉār kaṇṇaṉār 
āvūr kiḻār puṟanāṉūṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭum cāṉṟōrkaḷuḷ oruvar. āvūr eṉṟa peyaruṭiya vūrkaḷ tañci vaṭavārk kāṭu ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. āvūr vaṭavārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattut tiruvaṇṇāmali vaṭṭattu āvūrāka irukkalā meṉap puṟa nāṉūṟṟuṭ kūṟiṉām. avar makaṉārāṉa kaṇṇaṉār tam tantiyi yoppac ciṟanta nallicippulavarāka viḷaṅku kiṉṟār. yāṉi uyirttalāl vēṅkiyiṉ poṉṉiṟap pūkkaḷ utirtal, kollaṉ ulikkaḷattiṟ paḻukkak kāynta irumpi aṭikkumiṭattut teṟikkum pitirvukaṭku oppa viḷaṅkum eṉṟu kūṟumukattāl tamatu nuṇmāṇpulamiyit tām pāṭiya akappāṭṭoṉṟāl ivar veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟār. ivar pāṭiya pāṭṭu akanāṉūṟṟuḷ kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kaḷaviṉ kaṇ, kātal kimmikkut talimakaṉi iṉṟiyamiyāḷākiya talimakaḷatu mēṉikkaṇ tōṉṟiya vēṟupāṭu avaḷuṭiya cevilikku maṉakkalakkatti uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu. talivi yuḷḷattē muṟukiyirukkum kātali yuṇarāmal talimakaṉum variviṉi nīṭṭittu vantāṉ. inniliyil notumalar cilar uṇmiyaṟiyāmal talimakaḷ peṟṟōrpāl makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭi varuvārāyiṉar. ataṉi aṟinta tōḻikkut talivi yiṉ tamar talimakaṟku maṟuttu avarkaṭku makaṭkoṭi nērvar kol eṉṟa aiyam tōṉṟiṟṟu. uṭaṉē avaḷ talivi kuṟippuṇarntu taṉtāyākiya cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷā yiṉaḷ. tamiḻmakkaḷ vāḻvil peṇṭir oruvaṉi maṇattal tāṉ aṟameṉṟu kaṇṭiruntaṉar; “irumaṇam kūṭutal illiyal paṉṟu”1 eṉpar oruvaṟkut tamatu uḷḷatti nalkiṉ piṉpu ataṉi mīṭṭu vēṟoruvaṉpāl celuttār; tāṉ kātalitta āṇmakaṉukku vāḻkkip paṭṭavaḷ avaṉōṭē  uyir vāḻntu avaṉ iṟappiṉ avaṉōṭu uyirviṭutalō, aṉṟik kimmi mēṟkoṇṭu maṟupiṟappil avaṉiyē kaṇavaṉākap peṟutaṟku nōṟṟalō ceyvataṉṟi vēṟoruvaṉi maṇattal illi. iḵtu avarkaṭku aṟamātaliṉ, tām kātalitta āṇ makaṉiyaṉṟi  vēṟoruvaṉ tammi maṉiviyākap peṟal vēṇṭip peṟṟōrkaḷi makaṭkoṭi nērumāṟu kēṭpatu teriyumāyiṉ, uṭaṉē avarkaḷ tamakkuriya aṟattiṉkaṇ niṉṟu tāyar mutaliyōrkkut tam kātali yuṇarttuvar. atuvē aṟattoṭu  niṟṟal eṉpatu; aḵtu aṟattoṭunili yeṉavum vaḻaṅkum. avvāṟu niṟkumiṭattu makaḷir tamakkum kātalaṉākak koṇṭa āṇmakaṉukkum toṭarpuṇṭāya tiṟatti veḷippaṭiyākavum kuṟippākavum nilimik kēṟpa urippar. immuṟiyāl tōḻi kaḷaviṟkuc ciṟanta tāyātal paṟṟic ceviliyi aṇuki, “aṉṉāy, talimakaḷatu uḷḷattiṟ kōyilkoṇṭa talimakaṉatu tēr namatu pākkattukku nāṭōṟum vantu koṇṭirukkiṟatu; avaṉukkum talimakaṭkum uḷatāya kātaluṟavi ivvūravar alar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar; iṉi avar nam pākkattukkup pōntu vaṟitu peyarkuvārallar; itaṉi maṉattuṭ koṇṭu makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭi vantōri maṟuttal muṟiyām” eṉpatu paṭak kūṟalāṉāḷ. 
tōḻiyiṉ ikkūṟṟu, talivi talimakaṉpāl kātal koṇṭu uṟitali, avaṉ tērvaravu kāṭṭiyum avaṉatu kātaṟciṟappi avaṉatu tēr vaṟitu peyarkkuvataṉ ṟeṉṟu rittum pulappaṭuttal kaṇṭa kaṇṇaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār. ippāṭṭiṉ piṟpakuti ēṭukaḷil citintum vēṟupaṭṭum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. 
kaṇṭal vēlik kaḻicūḻ paṭappi 
muṇṭakam vēynta kuṟiyiṟik kurampik
koḻumīṉ koḷpavar pākkam kalleṉa
neṭuntēr paṇṇi varalā ṉātē
kuṉṟat taṉṉa kuvavumaṇal nīnti
vantaṉar peyarvarkol tāmē alkal
iḷiyarum mutiyarum kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīik
kōṭcuṟā eṟinteṉak1 kīṭpaṭac curuṅkiya
2muṭimutir valikik koṇṭu peruṅkaṭal 
3talikeḻu perumīṉ muṉṉiya
koliveñ ciṟāark 4kōṭpaṭ ṭaṉaḷē.
itu, notumalar varivu vantuḻit tōḻi cevilikku aṟat
toṭu niṉṟatu. 
uri : 
kaṇṭal vēlik kaḻicūḻ paṭappi  - kaṇṭalkaḷi vēliyākavuṭiya uppaṅkaḻikaḷāl cūḻappaṭṭa tōṭṭaṅ kālil; muṇṭakam vēynta kuṟiyiṟik kurampi - muḷḷi kaḷāl mēlē vēyappaṭṭa kuṟukiya kūriyiyuṭiya vīṭu kaḷil vāḻum; koḻumīṉ koḷpavar pākkam kalleṉa - koḻuviya mīṉkaḷip piṭikkum paratavaruṭiya ūrmuḻu vatum āravārikkumpaṭi; neṭuntēr paṇṇivaral āṉātu - kātalaruṭiya neṭiyatēr virinta celaviṟkamiyap paṇṇap paṭṭu nāḷum varuvatu tavirvatilli; kuṉṟattaṉṉakuvavu maṇal nīnti - kuṉṟu pōl niṟkum kuvinta maṇal mēṭukaḷik kaṭantu; vantaṉar peyarvarkol - ivaṇvantu cellum kātalar vaṟitu celvarkollō; aṟṟāyiṉ: alkal iḷiyarum mutiyarum kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīi - iravuppōtil iḷiyarum mutiyarum tattam cuṟṟattuṭaṉē kūṭi; kōṭcuṟā eṟinteṉak kīṭpaṭac curuṅkiya - kolivalla cuṟāmīṉi eṟintaṉarāka, ataṉāl tuṇṭākak kiḻikkappaṭṭuc curuṅkiya taṉāl; muṭimuti vali kikkoṇṭu - muṭikaḷ mikak koṇṭa valiyik kiyiṟkoṇṭu; peruṅkaṭal tali keḻu peru mīṉ muṉṉiya -  periya kaṭaliṭattē poruntiya perumīṉkaḷik karutic ceṉṟa; koliveñciṟārk kōṭpaṭṭaṉaḷ - mīṉkoli virumpum iḷiyavarāl koḷḷappaṭṭavaḷām e.ṟu. 
pākkam, kalleṉa, tēr paṇṇivaral āṉātu. nīnti vantaṉar peyarvarkol; peyarvarāyiṉ, kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīi eṟinteṉac karuṅkiya muṭimutir vali kikkoṇṭu, peruṅ kaṭal mīṉmuṉṉiya ciṟārk kōṭpaṭṭaṉaḷāvaḷ kāṇ eṉṟu kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. kaḻikkarikkum paṭappikkum iṭiyē kaṇṭalkaḷ vēliyāka niṉṟami tōṉṟak kaṇṭal vēlik kaḻicūḻ paṭappi eṉṟār. paṭappi, tōṭṭaṅkāl, muṇṭakam, kaḻimuḷḷi marutavippukkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum vēḻam pōla immuḷḷi vaki kaḻikkariyilum kaṭaṟkariyilum kāṭu pōl vaḷarntirukkum; ivaṟṟi aṟuttu āṇṭu vāḻum nuḷiyar tam vīṭukaṭkuk kūriyāka vēyvatu iyalpu. pākkam, neytaṉilattu ūr. perumaṇaṟparappil virintu cella vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, tērkkup paṇṇutal iṉṟiyamiyātā yiṟṟu. koli puriyum koṭumiyuṭimi paṟṟik kōṭcuṟā eṉṟār; kōḷ, īṇṭuk koli kuṟittatu. kīḷ. kiḻippuṇṭa tuṇṭu. cuṟāvāl aṟupaṭṭa valiyiṉi mikappala muṭiyiṭṭup payaṉ koḷḷumāṟu viḷaṅka muṭimutirvali eṉṟār. vali yiṟpaṭṭataṉi mutaṟkaṇ kolipurivatu iḷañciṟār ceyalā taliṉ, avarkaḷik koliveñciṟāar eṉṟār. kōṭ paṭṭaṉaḷ, etirmaṟik kuṟippu moḻi. 
neṭuntēr paṇṇi varalāṉātu eṉṟum, kuvavu maṇal nīnti vantaṉar peyarvar kol eṉṟum niṉṟa kūṟṟukkaḷāl iḵtu  aṟattoṭunili kaṇṇiya teṉpatu peṟappaṭum. tali makaḷip pōlat talimakaṉum avaḷi iṉṟiyamiyāp peruṅkātalaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ tērūrntu varutal tavirāṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvāḷ, pākkam kalleṉa neṭuntēr paṇṇi vālāṉātu eṉṟāḷ. avaṉ varivoṭu varutal orutali eṉṟaṟkup pākkam kalleṉa varal āṉātu eṉṟāḷumām. kuṟiyiṉ kaṇ uḷavāya iṭiyīṭukaḷ palavaṟṟiyum nīntip pōntu kūṭiya avaṉatu kātaṉmi pulappaṭa, kuṉṟattaṉṉa kuvavu maṇal nīnti vantaṉar eṉṟum, avarkku maṟuttu notumalarkku makaṭkoṭi nērvirāyiṉ avar muṉpōṟ peyarkuvārallar eṉṟum, uḷḷuṟiyāl notumalar variyiṉ talimakaḷ uyirvāḻāḷ eṉṟum, talimakaṉum iṟantu paṭuvataṉṟi uyirtāṅkik koṇṭu vaṟitu cellāṉ eṉpāḷ, vantaṉar peyarvarkol eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. iḷiyarum mutiyarum kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīic ceṉṟu kōṭcuṟā eṟintārāka, ataṉāl vali tuṇṭikkappaṭṭāṟpōlac cāṉṟōr pōntu makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭiṉārākat tamar maṟuttamiyāl varivu muṟintateṉṟum, kīṭpaṭac curuṅkiya muṭipala koṇṭa valiyik kikkoṇṭu peruṅkaṭaliṭattup perumīṉi muṉṉic cellum ciṟār pōla, makaḷmaṟuttami vāyilāka notumalar pōntu makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭukiṉṟaṉa reṉṟum, acciṟāarkkup perumīṉ akappaṭāvāṟu pōlat talimakaḷum avarkaḷāl variyappaṭāḷ eṉṟum, koliveñciṟār ākaliṉ, avar kippaṭuvaṉa iṟantupaṭutal pōlat talimakaḷum iṟantu paṭuvāḷ eṉṟum tōḻi uḷḷuṟutturittami kāṇka. 
viḷakkam : 
“nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum”1 eṉṟa nūṟpāviṉkaṇ, “piṟaṉ varivāyiṉum” eṉṟataṟku itaṉikkāṭṭi, itu notumalar varivu malintami tōḻi ciṟippuṟamākak kūṟiyatu eṉṟum,” itaṉuḷ “pāṟpaṭṭaṉaḷ eṉat teḷivupaṟṟi iṟanta kālattāṟ. kūṟiṉāḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
208. nocci niyamaṅ kiḻār
nocciniyamam eṉpatu kāviriyiṉ vaṭakari maḻanāṭṭu ūrkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. tiruccirāppaḷḷikku arukil kāviriyiṉ vaṭakariyil  ivvūr iṉṟum irukkiṟatu; ippōtu itaṉ peyar nocciyam eṉa maruviyuḷatu. iccāṉṟōratu iyaṟ peyar innūlkaḷ tokukkappaṭṭa kālattēyē maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. nocciniyamaṅkiḻār eṉṟa ciṟappu māttiram niṉṟa miyiṉ, tokuppāciriyar ataṉi maṭṭum kuṟittuḷḷār: akattuṟi poruṭṭuṟi eṉṟa iru tuṟiyilum iṉiya pāṭṭukkaḷ ivarāṟpāṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. vēṅkippūk koyyum makaḷir pulipuli eṉpatum, atukēṭṭu makkaḷ avarpāl celvatum, avarik koṇṭu makkaḷ eṭṭāta pūkkaḷik koytu koḷvatum piṟavum ivarāl kuṟikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. curuṅkac colliṉ ivaruṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ aṉittum ilakkiya vaḷam ceṟintavi yeṉiṉ atu miki yākātu.
maṉivāḻvil talimakaṉ maṉiviyiṟ pirintu ceṉ ṟu poruḷceyya vēṇṭiya kaṭamiyuṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. ataṉit talimakaḷ aṟiyiṉ pirivāṟṟāḷ eṉṟa accattāl avaṭku nēriṟ collāmal  kuṟippāl uṇarttiṉāṉ. ataṉi uṇarnta talimakaḷ āṟṟāḷāyt tōḻikku aṟivittu varuntiṉāḷ. ceyaṟkuriyavaṟṟic ceyyāmi āṇmakaṟku iḻukkātaliṉ, tōḻi talivi varuntal naṉṟaṉṟeṉpatik karuttuṭkoṇṭu, “tōḻi, nī iḷiyaḷātaliṉ vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpum talivaṉ maṉamāṇpum aṟiyāmal pirivu niṉintu kaṇṇīr corintu kiyaṟavupaṭukiṉṟāy. kātalar niṉṉiṟ pirintu celpavarallar; cella nēriṉum celaviṭit tōṉṟum pirivut tuṉpatti āṟṟum iyalpiṉarallar. niṉṉip peritum kātalippavarā taliṉ avar ciṟanta aṉpum meṉmiyum uṭiyar; mēlum pirivāṟṟāta nammiṉum avar tām mikka irakkamuṭiyava rātalāl, cellumiṭattu mēṟkoṇṭa poruḷ, kuṟitta poḻutiṉkaṇ muṭiyātāyiṉum, avar tavirātu vantu cērvar. orukāl avar maṟappiṉum, maḻimukil paṭarntuceṉṟu avarkkut tām vaṟpuṟutta poḻutiṉi niṉippittu uṭaṉē vantu kūṭacceyyumkāṇ” eṉṟu tēṟṟiṉāḷ.
tōḻiyiṉ itteḷippuriyiṉkaṇ, talimakaṉatu piriyā miyum pirivum pirintavaḻi āṟṟiyiruttaṟkuriya tiṟamum oruṅkē kūṟappaṭṭa oṭpaṅkaṇṭu viyanta nocciniyamaṅkiḻār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār’. 
‘1viṟalmiku viḷaṅkiḻi 2nekiḻac cāay
3aṟalavir nuṇṭuḷi yiṭimuli naṉippa
viḷivila 4kaliḻum kaṇṇoṭu peritaḻin
tevaṉiṉipu vāṭuti cuṭarnutaṟ kuṟamakaḷ
celvā rallarnaṅ kātalar 5celliṉum
nōṉmā rallar nōyē maṟṟavar
koṉṉum nampuṅ kuriyar tāmē
ciṟanta aṉpiṉar 6cāyalum uriyar
pirinta nammiṉum iraṅki arumporuḷ
muṭiyā tāyiṉum varuvar ataṉṟali
iṉṟuṇip pirintōr nāṭit
taruvatu pōlumip perumaḻik kuralē.
itu, celavuk kuṟippaṟintu āṟṟāḷāya talimakaṭkut tōḻi coṟṟatu.
uri : 
viṟalmiku viḷaṅkiḻi nekiḻac cāay - valimikku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa iḻikaḷ nekiḻntu nīṅkumāṟu melintu; aṟal avir nuṇtuḷi iṭimuli naṉippa - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nuṇṇiya kaṇṇīrttuḷikaḷ muliyakam naṉiyumpaṭi; viḷivila kaliḻum - nīṅkātu coriyum; kaṇṇoṭu peritu aḻintu - kaṇkaḷuṭaṉē mikavum varunti; evaṉ niṉipu vāṭuti - etaṟkāka nī avar pirivareṉa niṉintu vāṭu kiṉṟāy; cuṭarnutal kuṟumakaḷ - oḷiviḷaṅkum neṟṟiyiṉi yuṭiya iḷiyavaḷē; celvārallar nam kātalar - pirintu cellutalic ceyyārkāṇ, namatu kātalar; celliṉum - celvārāyiṉ; nōṉmār allar nōy - niṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟavaḻi eytum pirivut tuṉpatti avar poṟukka māṭṭār; avar koṉṉum nampum kuriyar - mēlum niṉṉip peritum kātalipparāvar; ciṟanta aṉpiṉar - niṉpāl mikac ciṟanta aṉpuṭiyavar; cāyalum uriyar - viriviṟ curuṅkum meṉmippaṇpum tamakku urimiyāka vuṭiyar; pirinta nammiṉum iraṅki - avarāl piriyappaṭṭa nammiṭattē peritum iraṅki; arumporuḷ muṭiyātā yiṉum - mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa arumporuḷ kuṟitta kālattu muṭiyātāyiṉum; varuvar - ataṉik karutātu tāṉ kuṟitta paruvavaravu karuti vantu cērvar; ataṉṟali - ataṉ mēlum; iṉṟuṇip pirintōr nāṭit taruvatu pōlum - iṉiya tuṇiyāyiṉārip pirintavar uṟiyumiṭatti nāṭic ceṉṟu avarik koṇarntu kūṭṭuvatu pōṉṟu uḷatu; ipperumaḻik kural - ipperiya maḻimukiliṉ muḻakkam, e-ṟu.
kuṟumakaḷ, iḻinekiḻac cāay, nuṇṭuḷi naṉippa, kaliḻum kaṇṇoṭu peritaḻintu, evaṉniṉipu vāṭuti; kātalar celvārallar; celliṉum avar nōy nōṉmā rallar; nampuṅkuriyar, aṉpiṉar, uriyar; poruḷ muṭiyātā yiṉum varuvar, maḻikkural taruvatu pōlum eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. eḷitil nekiḻāta valimi yuṭimi paṟṟi viṟaṉmiku viḷaṅkiḻi eṉṟār; “viṟaliḻip polinta kāṇpiṉ cāyal”1 coṭṭukiṉṟa nīr aṟal eṉappaṭum; annīrt tuḷiyi aṟalavir nuṇṭuḷi eṉṟār. nīrvārtal iṭiya ṟāmi tōṉṟa viḷivila kaliḻum kaṇṇeṉappaṭṭatu. maṟṟu, acinili, koṉ, perumiyāya mikuti kuṟittu niṉṟatu. kuri: acinili. perumiyum uraṉumēyaṉṟic cāyalum āṭavarkkuriya paṇpu; “vaṇaṅkiya cāyal vaṇaṅkā āṇmi”1  eṉpatu kāṇka. iṉṟuṇip pirintōr nāṭit taruvatu pōlum eṉṟatu tūtu ceṉṟu talivarik koṇarum iyalpiṟ ṟeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟatu.
talimakaṉ piriyak karutuvataṉik kuṟippāl aṟinta māttiriyē. talimakaṭku āṟṟāmiyum ataṉāl mēṉiyil vēṟupāṭum avalamum tōṉṟi varuttiṉamiyiṉ, ataṉi viḷaṅkiḻi nekiḻac cāay eṉṟatu, uṭampunaṉi curuṅkal, aṟalavir nuṇṭuḷi naṉippa eṉṟatu, aḻivil kūṭṭattu avaṉpirivāṟṟāmi. talimakaṉ kuṟippu nī karutiyavāṟu pirivu kaṇṇiya taṉṟu eṉṟaṟku, celvārallar nam kātalar eṉṟum, cellātāric celvareṉak karuti nī peritum varuntuvatu kūṭāteṉṟaṟkup peritaḻintu evaṉniṉipu vāṭuti eṉṟum, itaṟkuk kāraṇam niṉ iḷami yaṉṟi vēṟilli eṉpāḷ cuṭarnutal kuṟumakaḷ eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. tōḻiyi maṟuttut talimakaḷ kātalar kuṟippup pirivukaṇṇiya teṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉāḷāka, avaṭku, tōḻi, nī karutumāṟu avar celvarāyiṉ, akkāliyil tōṉṟi varuttum pirivut tuṉpatti avar poṟārākaliṉ iṭiyē maṭaṅki mīḷvar eṉpatu paṭa, colliṉum nōṉmārallar nōyē eṉṟāḷ. atukēṭkum talivi avar oṉṟit toṭaṅkiṉ iṭiyē maṭaṅkutal ilareṉṟāḷāka, avaṭkut tōḻi “aḵtu okkum; āyiṉum avarkkum niṉpāluḷḷa kātal peritu eṉpāḷ, avar koṉṉum nampum kuriyar eṉṟum, ciṟanta aṉpiṉar eṉṟum, taṉ kūṟṟāl talimakaṉ āṇmikku mācuṇṭākāmi nōkki, vaṇaṅkā āṇmiyum vaṇaṅkiya cāyalum uṭiya reṉpāḷ cāyalum uriyar eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. cāyalum eṉṟa ummiyāl āṇmiyurimi taḻuvikkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. aḵtu iyalpātaliṉ, poruḷ karutic ceṉṟamiyiṉ avar pāluḷḷa aṉpum cāyalum avarcelaviṉit taṭuttu niṟuttum matuki yuṭiyavākā eṉṟu talivi karutiṉāḷāka, poruḷiṉum avarkku nampāl uḷatāya aruḷ periteṉpāḷ, pirinta nammiṉum iraṅki eṉṟum, ataṉāl poruḷ muṭiyātā yiṉum varuvar eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. muṭiyātāyiṉum eṉṟatu, mēṟkoṇṭa poruḷi muṭiyātoḻiyār eṉpatip peṟuvittatu. ettiṟattārāyiṉum taṉṉi nāṭiyavaḻit taṉakku aṭimippaṭutti avaratu uḷḷattip piṇikkum peruvaṉmi poruṭku uṇṭātaliṉ, ataṉic ceyvōr niṉivum ceyalum ataṉpāl oṉṟutaliṉ, avar tām kuṟitta poḻutil tavaṟātu varuvateṉṟaṟku vāyppilli eṉṟu kūṟiya talivikku, orukāl avar maṟappārāyiṉ, avar kuṟikkum kārpparuva varavi maḻi mukil eḻuntu ceṉṟu taṉ iṭikkural kāṭṭip pirintuṟiyum avaruṭiya kātaliyi niṉippikkum eṉpāḷ, iṉṟuṇip pirintōr nāṭit taruvatu pōlum ip perumaḻik kural eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. eṉavē, kātalar pirivārallar; piriyiṉum kuṟitta poḻutil vārātoḻiyār; orukāl avar maṟappiṉum maḻimukil tūtu ceṉṟu avarik koṇarum eṉṟu tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyavāṟu. itaṉāl talivi āṟṟiyiruppāḷāvatu payaṉ.
209. kaccippēṭṭu naṉṉākiyār
ivarpeyar kacci naṉṉākiyār eṉṟu tēvar ēṭṭil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  accuppaṭiyil ippāṭṭip pāṭiyavar nocciniyamaṅkiḻār eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatu, ēṭupeyart teḻutap peṟṟapōtu nērnta piḻi yākalām. kāñcimānakariṉ oru pakutikkuc kaccippēṭu eṉpatu peyar; iḵtu iṭikkālak kalveṭṭukkaḷilum1 kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. naṉṉākaṉār eṉpatu āṭavarik kuṟippatu pōla, naṉṉākiyār eṉṟa ippeyar peṇ pālārkkuriyatākat tōṉṟutaliṉ ivar oru nallicip pulami melliyalār eṉṟu teḷivākiṟatu. ivar pāṭiyaṉa vākac cila pāṭṭukkaḷ kuṟuntokiyil uḷḷaṉa.
iyaṟkippuṇarcci vakiyāl taliviyiṉ kātaluṟavu peṟṟa talimakaṉ, avaḷuṭiya uyirttōḻiyiṉ naṭpup peṟṟālaṉṟit tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kātaloḻukkam iṉitiṉ iyalāteṉpatu teḷintu avaḷuṭiya naṭpip peṟa muyal vāṉāyiṉaṉ; puṉaṅkāval mēṟkoṇṭu tiṉikkolliyil talimakaḷum tōḻiyum iruppatu kaṇṭu aṅkē ceṉṟu tōḻiyoṭu uri nikaḻttuvāṉāyt taṉ  kātaṟkuṟippum taliviyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyum kuṟippāy uṇarttiṉāṉ. taliviyiṉ maṉakkuṟippit tāṉ aṟiyāmiyālum tali makaṉatu uḷḷat tuṇmiyi naṉku uṇarāmiyālum tōḻi neñcil accameytit talimakaṉi maṟuttuc cēṭpaṭuk kaluṟṟāḷ. ataṉāl kiyaṟavuṟṟuk kalaṅkiya talimakaṉ, tōḻiyiṉ arukē niṉṟu avaḷ cevippaṭumāṟu taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟuvāṉāy, “neñcē, nam uyirvāḻkkikku uṟutuṇiyum nām eytivaruntum vēṭkinōykku maruntumākiya tali makaḷ cila vitikaḷi vittip pala viḷivittukkoḷḷum kuṟavaruṭiya tiṉippuṉattē irukkiṉṟāḷ, avaḷuṭiya iṉiya coṟkaḷi iṅkut tiṉikavarvāṉ varum kiḷikaḷ naṉku aṟintuḷḷaṉa; avi eṉ cevippaṭiṉ eṉ varuttamellām oḻintupōkiṉṟaṉa; avaḷuṭiya collōci eṉcevi kēḷātu oṭuṅkumāyiṉ, eṉ uyirum uṇarvum ceyalum yāvum oḻintupōkiṉṟaṉa; it tōḻi māttiram ēṉ ivaṟṟi uṇarā toḻikiṉṟāḷ?” eṉa urittāṉ.
talimakaṉuṭiya ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talimakaṭkum taṉakkum uḷatāya uṟaviṉip pulappaṭuttit tāṉ avaḷi iṉṟiyami yāṉātaliyum tōḻi ataṉiyuṇarntu tuṇipurital vēṇṭu meṉpatiyum kuṟippākavum teḷivākavum talimakaṉ pulap paṭuttiya nayam kaṇṭa kaccippēṭṭu naṉṉākiyār ip
pāṭṭiṉkaṇ ataṉi amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
maliyiṭam paṭuttuk kōṭṭiya kollit
taḷipatam1 peṟṟuk kāṉuḻu kuṟavar
cilavit takala2 vittip palaviḷit
tiṟaṅkukural piṟaṅkiya ēṉal uḷḷāḷ
maḻaliyaṅ kuṟumakaḷ miḻaliyan3 tīñcol
kiḷḷiyum tāmaṟi pavvē eṉakkē
1paṭuṅkāṟ piyuḷ tīrum paṭāatu
taviruṅ kāli yāyiṉeṉ
uyirō ṭellām uṭaṉvāṅ kummē.
itu, kuṟi maṟukkappaṭṭup piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṉ āṟṟā ṉāyt taṉṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu. 2tōḻi kēṭṭuk kuṟi muṭip pāḷāvatu payaṉ.
uri : 
maliyiṭam paṭuttuk kōṭṭiya kolli - malippak kattē iṭam kaṇṭu varampiṭṭu vaḷittup paṇpaṭuttiya kolliyil; taḷipatam peṟṟuk kāṉuḻu kuṟavar - maḻi peytamiyāl uḻavukkuriya cevvi vāykkappeṟṟu ataṉi uḻutu payaṉkoḷḷum iyalpiṉarākiya kuṟavar; cila vittu akala vitti - cilavāya vitikaḷik koṇṭu vittukkaṭ kiṭiyē iṭamuṇṭākat tūvi vitittu; (uṭaṉ) pala viḷittu iṟaṅku kural piṟaṅkiya ēṉal uḷḷāḷ - uṭaṉaṭi yākap pala tiṉikaḷi viḷittuk katir cāyntu uyarntu niṟkum tiṉippayir vaḷarnta puṉattiṉkaṇṇē iruppava ḷākiya; maḻaliyaṅ kuṟumakaḷ - maḻali māṟāta iḷiya vaḷuṭiya; miḻaliyan tīñcol - pēcciṭit tōṉṟum iṉiya coṟkaḷi; kiḷḷiyum tām aṟipa - mēyvāṉ varum kiḷikaḷum naṉku aṟintuḷḷaṉa; eṉakkup paṭuṅkāl piyuḷ tīrum - eṉ ceviyiṟpaṭumpōtu, eṉpāl tōṉṟi varuttum vēṭkinōy nīṅkiviṭukiṟatu; paṭāatu taviruṅ kāliyāyiṉ - ceviyiṟpaṭātu oṭuṅkumāyiṉ; eṉ uyirōṭu ellām uṭaṉvāṅkum - eṉ āruyirōṭu aṟivu cālpu mutaliya āṇmippaṇpukaḷ aṉittum nīṅkāniṟkum. ittōḻi aṟiyātu cēṭpaṭukkiṉṟāḷākaliṉ yāṉ eṅṅaṉam āṟṟuvēṉ? e.ṟu.
kuṟavar, kolliyil taḷipatam peṟṟuc cilavittu akala vittip pala viḷittaliṉ, kural piṟaṅkiya ēṉal uḷḷāḷ kuṟumakaḷ; avaḷuṭiya tīñcol kiḷiyum aṟipa; eṉakkup paṭuṅkāṟ piyuḷ tīrum; paṭāatu tavirumāyiṉ eṉ uyirōṭu ellām uṭaṉ vāṅkum;tōḻi itaṉi aṟiyātu cēṭpaṭuk kiṉṟāḷākaliṉ eṅṅaṉam yāṉ āṟṟuvēṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. maliyiṉkaṇ tiṉi viḷitaṟkuriya iṭamkaṇṭu kolli yamikka vēṇṭutaliṉ maliyiṭam paṭuttu eṉṟum, avviṭatti varampiṭṭu uḻavēṇṭutaliṉ kōṭṭiya kolli eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. varampiṭṭu vaḷip patu paṟṟik kōṭṭiya eṉṟār. kōṭṭam, vaḷivu. maḻi peyyiṉum uḻavukkuriya cevvi vēṟu tōṉṟa vēṇṭu mākalāṉ taḷi patam peṟṟuk kāṉuḻu kuṟavar eṉṟār. iṭampaṭuttuk kōṭṭam amittaṟku muṉ avviṭam kāṉamāka iruntamiyiṉ kāṉ eṉṟār. iṉṟum pāṇṭināṭṭavarum koṅkunāṭṭavarum puṉceyk kollikaḷik kāṭu eṉpatē peruvaḻakku. akala vittal, vittiṭum muṟi, vittiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟum payirkaṭkiṭiyē kāṟṟum ñāyiṟṟoḷiyum naṉku nilavumāṟu amintālaṉṟi viḷivu nalamum perukkamum eytāmiyiṉ akala vittal muṟiyāyiṟṟu; āḻavuḻutu akala viti eṉpatu uḻavupaṟṟiya ulakuriyākum. oru vitiyil tōṉṟiya payir pala maṇikaḷi viḷittaliṉ, cilavittu akalavittip palaviḷittu  eṉṟār. vitti, vittiyataṉāl eṉka. katiriṭattēyuḷḷa maṇikaḷiṉ poṟiyāṟṟātu tali cāytal iyalpu. maḻali, īṇṭu maḻali moḻi vaḻaṅkutaṟkuriya iḷami kuṟittu niṉṟatu. miḻali, miḻaṟṟappaṭum col. aṟipa, akaravīṟṟup palavaṟicol. piyuḷ, varuttam. uyir eṉṟavaḻi ataṟkuriya aṟivu niṟi cālpu mutaliya paṇpukaḷum aṭaṅku taliṉ, ellām eṉṟu oḻintār. vāṅkutal, koṇṭucēṟal; vaḷittalumām.
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam peṟa muyalukiṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉṉāṟ karutik kūṟappaṭum talimakaḷit tōḻi naṉku uṇarntu kōṭaṟporuṭṭu iṟaṅkukural piṟaṅkiya ēṉaluḷḷāḷ eṉṟāṉ. iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉum vaḻinilik kāṭciyiṉum talimakaḷ taṉittut talik kūṭiyapōtu avaḷ uritta coṟkaḷ nāṇa mikutiyāl arikuṟiyāy aṉpu niṟin tiruntamiyiṉ maḻaliyaṅ kuṟumakaḷ eṉṟum, puṉattiṉkaṇ kiḷiyōppuṅ kālattu eḻum ōciyiṉkaṇ nāṇamō piṟar kāṇpa reṉṟa accamō iṉṟi iyalpāy iṉimi mikkirut taliṉ ataṉi miḻaliyan tīñcol eṉṟum, avaḷ kuraleṭuttu ōppiyavaḻi avaḷ collōci kiḷikaṭkut tammiṉattiṉ ōcipōlat tōṉṟiṉamiyiṉ avi ciṟitum accamiṉṟi iṉitiruntuṇṭal paṟṟik kiḷḷiyum tām aṟipavvē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. taṭṭiyum kavaṇiyum koṇṭavaḻi nīṅkutalum, kuraleṭuttu ōppiyavaḻi nīṅkāmi yum kaṇṭu iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāṉ eṉṟumām. avaḷi meyyuṟak kāṭṭic ceviyāl colkēṭṭu iṉpuṟuvittaliṉ, kāṇā vaḻip piṟakkum tuṉpam nīṅkutalāl, eṉakkup paṭuṅkāl piyuḷ tīrum eṉṟum, uṇṭal uṟaṅkal mutaliya kālaṅkaḷil kiḷiyōppal tavirtaliṉ akkāliyil puṉattukkup pōnta vaḻit tāṉ avaḷik kāṇṭaliṉṟip peruntuya ruḻantu kiyaṟavu paṭutali, paṭātu taviruṅkāli āyiṉ eṉṟum, eṉ uyirōṭu ellām uṭaṉ vāṅkummē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. “iṅṅaṉam taliviyatu kāṭciyum collum eṉ maṉakkavali pōkki iṉpuṟuttuvatiyum, kāṇāmiyum coṟkēṭṭa liṉmiyum eṉ aṟivu niṟi āṇmikaḷōṭu kūṭiya eṉ uyiri vāṅki varuttutaliyum aṟiyātu ittōḻi eṉṉi maṟuttuc cēṭpaṭukkiṉṟāḷ. iṉi eṅṅaṉam āṟṟu vēṉ” eṉpatu kuṟippeccam. ataṉāṟpayaṉ, tōḻi kēṭṭuk kuṟimuṭippāḷāvatu. taḷipatam peṟṟuk kāṉuḻu kuṟavar cilavāya vitiyiṉi vittup palavāya tiṉiyiṉi viḷittuk kōṭal pōla, kaṭṭiḷamiyum kāmac cevviyum peṟṟuḷḷa ivaḷ cilavāya taṉ coṟkaḷic collip palavāy māṇpuṟṟa eṉ kuṇanalaṅkaḷiyum kavarntukoṇṭāḷ eṉat talimakaṉ uḷḷuṟiyāl taṉakkum talimakaṭkum iṭiyē uṇṭākiya uṟavi muṉṉuṟa vuṇarttit tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟṟu niṉṟami kuṟittavāṟu kāṇka.


210. miḷi kiḻāṉ nalvēṭṭaṉār
maṉiyaṟam purintoḻukum talimakaṉpāl puṟat toḻukka muṇṭākavē, māṉam poṟāta māṇpuṭiya talimakaḷ peritum maṉam puḻuṅki mēṉi vēṟupaṭalāṉāḷ. parattiyar pāl taliniṉṟoḻukiya talimakaṉ ulakiyalpaṟṟit taṉ
maṉikku varuvāṉāyp pāṇaṉ mutaliya vāyilkaḷit talimakaḷpāl celuttiṉāṉ. avar aṉivariyum talimakaḷ vāyiṉ maṟuttu ōṭṭiṉāḷ. muṭivil, talimakaṉ tāṉē tōḻiyik kaṇṭu taṉ poruṭṭu vāyil vēṇṭumāṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭāṉ. talimakaṉ nērmukamākap pōnta viṭattu vāyiṉ maṟuttal tōḻikku aṟamaṉṟu; avaṟku orukāl talimakaḷ vāyiṉ maṟuppaḷāyiṉ tōḻi avaṭku ilvāḻviṉ nallaṟam kūṟit talimakaṟku vāyil nērvippatutāṉ muṟiyum kaṭamiyu mākum. talimakkaḷiṭiyē, aṟivum aṟamum aṉpum tiraṇṭu peṇvaṭiviṟ pōntāṟ pōlum perun takavuṭiya tōḻi iruppatu, iruvar uḷḷaṅkaḷum vēṟupaṭṭup piṇaṅkumiṭattup piṇakku nīkki oṉṟupaṭutti iṉpam peṟu vittaṟ poruṭṭē eṉpatu akanūṟ karuttu. anneṟimivaḻi yoḻukum tōḻi, talimakaṉ vāyil vēṇṭum kuṟippiṉaṉā tali yuṇarntatum, avaṉ peṟaṟkariya māṇpukaḷi uḷḷuṟi yāṟ kuṟittu, veḷippaṭiyāka mikka periya celvaṉāṉa avaṉatu celvatti vitantu kūṟi, “ūraṉē, neṭiya moḻi talum kaṭiya vūrtalum celvamalla; avi avaravar ceyviṉip payaṉ eṉṟum, cērntōrkku uḷavākum tuṉpam tuṭittu iṉpampeṟat tuṇipurivataṉiyē celvam eṉṟum collupa cāṉṟōr; ataṉiyē yāṉ kēṭṭuḷēṉ; ākalāṉ, niṉṉi yiṉṟi amiyāḷāy niṉṉic cērntuṟiyum talimakaḷ niṉpirivāl eytum tuṉpattit tuṭikka niṉiyātoḻuku tal naṉṟaṉṟu” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.
tōḻiyatu ivvuriyiṉkaṇ miḷirum aṟavuriyik kaṇṭu viyappumikka miḷikiḻāṉ nalvēṭṭaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
arikāl māṟiya aṅkaṇ akaṉvayal maṟukāl uḻuta īrac1 ceṟuvayiṉ
vittoṭu ceṉṟa vaṭṭi paṟpala
mīṉoṭu peyarum yāṇar ūra
neṭiya moḻitalum kaṭiya vūrtalum
celva maṉṟu1tam ceyviṉip payaṉeṉac
2collupa eṉpa cāṉṟōr cērntōr
puṉkaṇ añcum paṇpiṉ
meṉkaṇ 3celvam celvam eṉpatuvē.
itu talimakaṉi neruṅkic colluvāḷāyt tōḻi vāyil nērvāḷ kūṟiyatu.
uri : 
arikāl māṟiya  aṅkaṇ akalvayal - nelliṉ arikāl uḻavāl nīkkappaṭṭa iṭamakaṉṟa naṉcey vayaliṉ kaṇ; maṟukāl uḻuta īrac ceṟuvayiṉ - maṟucāl uḻuta taṉāl īram pularāta ceṟuvil vitittaṟku; vittoṭu ceṉṟa vaṭṭi - uyarnta vitikaḷik koṇṭu ceṉṟa kūṭikaḷ; paṟpala mīṉoṭu peyarum yāṇar ūra - palavēṟu vakippaṭṭa iḻinta mīṉkaḷik koṇṭu mīḷum putumi yaṟāta ūraṉē; neṭiya moḻitalum - collum ceyalum vēṟupaṭāmi vaṟpuṟuttum āṇmi moḻikaḷi vaḻaṅku vatum; kaṭiya ūrtalum - virinta celaviyuṭiya kutiri tēr mutaliyaṉa ūrntu celuttuvatum; celvam aṉṟu tam ceyviṉippayaṉ eṉa - celvamākā eṉavum tām tām ceyta viṉippayaṉāl viḷivaṉa eṉavum; collupa eṉpa cāṉṟōr - uyarntōr colluvar eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟuvar; cērntōr puṉkaṇ añcum paṇpiṉ - tammic cērntōratu varuttaṅkaṇṭu añcum paṇpoṭu kūṭiya; meṉkaṇ celvam - aruḷuṭimiyākiya celvamē; celvam eṉpatu - celvameṉa avarkaḷāl ciṟappittuc collappaṭuvatu, itaṉi maṟantu nī ivaḷi niṉ puṟat toḻukkattāṟ pirintu varuttuvatu kūṭātukāṇ e ṟu.
ūra, moḻitalum ūrtalum celvamaṉṟu, ceyviṉip payaṉ eṉac cāṉṟōr collupa eṉpa; cērntōr puṉkaṇ añcum paṇpiṉ meṉkaṇ celvam celva meṉpatu; nī ataṉi maṟantu puṟattiṟ pirintu ivaḷi varuttuvatu kūṭātu kāṇ eṉak kūṭṭi, viṉimuṭivu ceyka. arikāl, nellariyap paṭṭavaḻi nilattil niṟkum ataṉuṭiya aṭippakuti. āḻa vuḻumiṭattu arikāl vērōṭē peyarntu nīṅkuvatu paṟṟi, uḻutavayal arikāl māṟiya vayal eṉappaṭṭatu. nel viḷiyum naṉcey vayal eṉpatu tōṉṟa aṅkaṇ akalvayal eṉṟār. puḻuti paṭumāṟu orukālikkup palakāl uḻutal vēṇṭutaliṉ maṟukāl uḻuta ceṟuvayiṉ eṉṟum, ataṉkaṇ nīrāl uṇṭāya īram virivil māṟātākaliṉ īracceṟu eṉṟum kuṟittār. muṟṟi viḷinta neṉmaṇikaḷuḷ pataṭiyum karukkāyum pōkkic ciṟantaṉavāka vuḷḷavaṟṟi vitikkeṉṟu koḷpavākaliṉ  “uyarnta viti koṇṭu ceṉṟa vaṭṭi” eṉṟum, uyarnta vitiyi nōkka mīṉ iḻintamiyiṉ “iḻinta mīṉ” eṉṟum uri kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. vaṭṭi, kūṭi; kaṭakappeṭṭi eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. yāṇar, putumi; īṇṭup putuvaruvāy mēṟṟu. neṭiya moḻital, neṭumoḻi kūṟal; “iṉṉatu ceyyēṉāyiṉ iṉṉa ṉākuval” eṉa āṇmi tōṉṟak kūṟum vañciṉam; colliyavāṟē ceyval eṉac collum ceyalum oppa oḻukum tiṇmiyuṭimi yāppuṟuttaṟku vaḻaṅkappaṭum. kaṭi, virivu. aṟivu, āṇmi, poruḷ, paṭi mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟantōrpāl celvam cērtaliṉ, avaṟṟiyuṭimiyē celvamām eṉpatu paṟṟic celvam eṉṟār. aṉṟeṉpatit taṉittaṉik kūṭṭuka. puṉkaṇ, tuṉpam; meṉkaṇ, aruḷ. aruḷic celvameṉac ciṟappittu “aruṭcelvam celvattuṭ celvam”1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. ēṉip poruṭcelvam uyarntōrpālēyaṉṟi iḻinta kīḻmakkaḷpālum uḷatākalum, aruṭcelvam uyarntōrpālaṉṟip piṟar pāl uḷatā kāmiyum nōkki, aruḷē celvameṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu eṉa aṟika.
illiruntu nallaṟam purintoḻukum talimakaṉ puṟat toḻukip pōntakāli avaṟku mutaṟkaṇ vāyil maṟukkap paṭṭataṉāl, muṭivil avaṉē pōntu tōḻiyi vāyil vēṇṭiṉāṉāka, avaṟku nērtaliyaṉṟi maṟukkum tiṟam tōḻikku illi; āyiṉum, avaṉatu parattimiyi maṟuttal iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉ, ataṉ payaṉiṉmiyi veḷippaṭak kūṟal muṟiyaṉmi kaṇṭu uḷḷuṟut turittaliṉ, veḷip paṭiyil, āṇmi yētuvāka neṭumoḻi puṇarttalum, poruḷuṭimi yētuvākak kaṭiyavūrtalum celvamākā eṉpāḷ. neṭiya moḻitalum kaṭiya vūrtalum celvamaṉṟu eṉṟāḷ. “viṉinalam vēṇṭiya ellām tarum”1 eṉṟuṇarntu, ataṉkaṇ tiṇṇiyarāy2 niṉṟu, ollum vāyellām iruḷ tīra eṇṇi3 iṉpamum pukaḻum payappaṉavaṟṟic ceyvatu īṇṭuc ceyviṉi eṉappaṭṭatu. aṟivum āṇmiyum poruḷ paṭikaḷāl ākiya perumiyu mākiya mūṉṟālum uḷavākum celvam ētuvum payaṉumāy iyintu niṟṟaliṉ, ceyviṉip payaṉ eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟiṉar eṉka. parantu yarnta aṟivuṭiyāḷ pōlat tōḻi talimakaṉ muṉṉiṉṟu kūṟāḷātaliṉ, cāṉṟōrmēl vittuc collupa eṉpa cāṉṟōr eṉṟum, avarkaḷāṟ ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum akkūṟṟiṉit tāṉ koṇṭu kūṟumāṟṟāl, talimakaṉ uḷattē talivipāl aṉpu curuṅkiṉami kāṭṭi, ataṉi vilakki mikka aṉpuṭiyaṉātal vēṇṭumeṉa vaṟpuṟutti vāyil nērkiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, cērntōr puṉkaṇ añcum paṇpiṉ, meṉkaṭ celvam celvam eṉpatuvē” eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “parattimi maṟuttal vēṇṭiyum kiḻatti, maṭattaku kiḻami yuṭimi yāṉum, aṉpili koṭiyi eṉṟalum uriyaḷ”4 eṉṟum, “uyarmoḻik kiḷaviyum uriyavāl avaṭkē”5 eṉṟum āciriyar tōḻikku urimi vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. talivipāl aṉpiliyāyp pirintu, parattamiyāl uḷḷam kōṭiya koṭumiyāl talimakaṭkut tuṉpam viḷi vittatōṭu ataṉik kāṇṭalum ceyyāyāyiṉi eṉpāḷ, cērntōr puṉkaṇ añcum meṉkaṇ illiyāyiṉi eṉṟāḷ. ulakiyaṉmēl vittup potuppaṭa moḻitaliṉ, cērntōḷ eṉṉātu cērntōr eṉṟāḷ. īrac ceṟuvayiṉ uyarnta vittoṭu ceṉṟa vaṭṭi, ataṉāl peṟalākum viḷipayaṉip peṟumuṉ, iḻinta pal vēṟu mīṉkaḷik koṭupōntāṟ pōla, uyarnta taliviyoṭu kūṭi illiṉ kaṇ nallaṟañ ceyyappukka nī ataṉ payaṉip peṟumuṉ parattiyar palarpāṟ peṟalākum iḻinta payaṉip peṟāniṉṟaṉi eṉa uḷḷuṟi koḷka. īttuḻip pukaḻ payakkum nelpōla, mīṉ, koṭikkuri marapiṉ pukaḻ payavāmiyiṉ iḻintatām. kulamakaḷir pōlap parattiyar makappayantu payaṉpaṭārākaliṉ, iḻintavarāyiṉar; ‘avarum, pintoṭi makaḷiroṭu ciṟuvarp payantu, naṉṟi cāṉṟa kaṟpōṭu, empāṭātal ataṉiṉu maritē’1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. talimakaṉ neṭiya moḻintatu, parattiyaroṭu cūḷceytu  kūṭiyatu; kaṭiyavūrntatu, paratti yarit tēriṟ koṇarntu kūṭiyatu.
211. kōṭṭiyūr nallantiyār
ivar peyar koṭṭiyūr nallentiyār eṉṟum koṭiyūr nalvantiyār eṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. cēramāṉ enti eṉṟoru cāṉṟōr kuṟuntokiyuṭ kāṇappaṭutalāl, koṭṭiyūr nallentiyār eṉpatum poruttamākavuḷatu. kōṭṭiyūr pāṇṭināṭṭuc ciṟappuṭiya ūrkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. nallaṉ tanti eṉpatu nallanti eṉṟu vantatu eṉpar. vēṟu tokinūlkaḷuḷ ivaruṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ kāṇappaṭa villi.
talimakaḷik kaḷaviṟkūṭi iṉpuṟṟuvarum talimakaṉ kātal ciṟattal vēṇṭi variyātu nīṭṭittuvantāṉ. avaṉ uḷḷattē varivu paṟṟiya niṉivu tōṉṟuvatu kuṟittut tōḻi kuṟippākat takka coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉāḷ. talimakaṉō kaḷaviyē virumpiyoḻukiṉāṉ. taliviyi avaḷatu niḻal pōl toṭarntu avaḷ uḷḷak kuṟippukkaḷi uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ uṇarntoḻukum tōḻi talimakaṉ varivi nīṭṭippatu talimakaṭku mikka āṟṟāmi viḷivittu varuttumeṉa eṇṇi neñcil naṭukkamuṟa lāṉāḷ. naṭuṅkiya neñcam naṭuvu piṟaḻntatu; ākātaṉa pala niṉivukaḷ avaḷ neñci alittaṉa. “talimakaṉ talimakaḷik kaḷaviṟ kaṇṭu naṭpuṟṟu iṉpuṟutti yavaṉ ataṉi maṟantu avaḷik kiviṭṭut tuṟantāṉ pōlum; ataṉāl varintu kōṭali niṉiyātu nīṭṭikkiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟu niṉittāḷ.  uḷḷam kalaṅkiṟṟu. anniliyil tali makaṉ talivi maṉiyiṉ orupuṟattē vantu niṉṟāṉ. avaṉ taṉṉik kāṇātavāṟu avaṉ niṉṟa cūḻalil tāṉ urippaṉa avaṉ ceviyiṟ paṭumaḷavil ceṉṟu tōḻi taṉakkut tāṉē uriyāṭaluṟṟu, “tuṟikeḻu koṇkaṉākiya talimakaṉ taliviyi maṟantu tuṟantāṉ eṉpati yāriṭam colli varuntuvēṉ? evarik kāṇiṉum uḷḷam peritum añcu kiṟatu” eṉṟu viḷampiṉāḷ.
avvuri taṉatu talivipāl kātal kimmikku āṟṟāmi mēmpaṭṭu niṉṟami kaṇṭa tōḻi, talimakaṉip paṉmuṟi varivukaṭāviyum avaṉ ataṉi niṉiyātu nīṭṭittatu avaḷi avaṉ tuṟantāṉ eṉpatik kāṭṭiṉamiyum, ataṉāl tāṉ ataṉip piṟar evariṭattum urikkalākāmi niṉintu varuntiṉamiyum veḷippaṭuttum tiṟaṅkaṇṭa nallantiyār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
yārkkunon turikkō yāṉē ūrkaṭal
ōtañ ceṉṟa uppuṭic ceṟuvil
koṭuṅkaḻi maruṅkiṉ irivēṭ ṭeḻunta
1karuṅkāṟ kurukiṉ kōḷuyntu pōkiya
muṭaṅkupuṟa iṟaviṉ mōvāy ēṟṟi
2eṟitiri tokutta ekkar neṭuṅkōṭṭut
3tuṟumaṭal taliya tōṭupoti tāḻi
vaṇṭupaṭu 4vāṉpōtu verūum
tuṟikeḻu koṇkaṉ tuṟantaṉa ṉeṉavē.
itu, varivunīṭa orutali āṟṟāḷāmeṉṟa tōḻi ciṟippuṟa
mākat taṉṉuḷḷē colliyatu.
uri : 
yārkku yāṉ nontu urikkō - yāvarpāl yāṉ talimakaṉ ceyaṟku nontu urippēṉ; ūr kaṭal ōtam ceṉṟa uppuṭic ceṟuviṉ - paranta kaṭaliṉ perukkuc ceṉṟu pāyntataṉāl uppu viḷiyum pāttiyiṉkaṇ; koṭuṅkaḻi maruṅkiṉ irivēṭṭu eḻunta - vaḷinta kaḻiyiṭattiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu mēyum mīṉkaḷi vēṭṭi yāṭit tiṉṟaṟ keḻunta; karuṅkāl kurukiṉ kōḷ uyntu pōkiya - kariya kālkaḷiyuṭiya nīrkkurukiṉuṭiya piṭikku akappaṭṭut tappi uyntupōṉa; muṭaṅkupuṟa iṟaviṉ mōvāy ēṟṟi - vaḷinta mutukiyuṭiya iṟāmīṉiṉ nīṇṭa vīciyiyuṭiya āṟṟalmikka āṇ; eṟitiri tokutta ekkar neṭuṅkōṭṭu - mōtukiṉṟa alikaḷāl koḻikkap paṭṭa ekkar maṇaliṉ neṭiya kariyil vaḷarntuḷḷa; tuṟumaṭal taliya tōṭu poti tāḻi - neruṅkiya maṭa liṭattu itaḻkaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭut tōṉṟum tāḻiyiṉ; vaṇṭu paṭu vāṉpōtu verūum- vaṇṭu moykkum veṇmiyāṉa pūmpōtiṉik kaṇṭu veṇkurukeṉak karuti añcum; tuṟikeḻu koṇkaṉ tuṟantaṉaṉ eṉa - tuṟivaṉākiya talimakaṉ emmik kiviṭṭut tuṟantāṉ eṉṟu e.ṟu.
kurukiṉ kōḷuyntu pōkiya iṟaviṉ ēṟṟi ekkar neṭuṅkōṭṭut tāḻi vāṉpōtu verūum tuṟikeḻu koṇkaṉ tuṟantaṉaṉ eṉa yārkku nontu yāṉ urikkō eṉakkūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. urikku, taṉmi viṉimuṟṟu. ō. acinili. ūrkaṭal, ūrtal, parattal, ōtam, kaṭaṟperukku, kurukiṉ uṭal veḷuttum kāl kaṟuttum iruttaliṉ, karuṅkāṟ kuruku eṉṟār. kōḷ, kōṭal, iṟāmīṉiṉ mēṟpuṟam vaḷintu mutuku kōṇaliṉ muṭaṅku puṟa iṟavu eṉṟār. “iruṅkaḻi mukanta ceṅkō lavvali. muṭaṅkupuṟa iṟavōṭu iṉamīṉ ceṟikkum”1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. mōvāy, vīci; tāṭiyumām. “puṉṟāḷ veḷḷeli mōvā yēṟṟi”2 eṉpatu kāṇka. āṟṟaloṭu puṇarnta āṇpāli ēṟṟi eṉṟal muṟi eṉpar āciriyar. valimikac ceṟinta maṭal eṉṟaṟkut tuṟumaṭal eṉṟār. vāṉpōtu, veṇmiyāṉa arumpu, tāḻiyiṉpū veṇkuruku pōlat tōṉṟutali, “tayaṅkutiri poruta tāḻi veṇpūk, kurukeṉa malarum”1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. eṉa veṉṉum iṭiccol īṇṭu viṉi yāyiṟṟu. “viṉiyē kuṟippē iciyē paṇpē, eṇṇē peyarōṭu avvaṟu kiḷaviyum, kaṇṇiya nilittē eṉaveṉ kiḷavi”2 eṉṟu āciriyar urippatu kāṇka.
