kaḷavuk kātaloḻukkam pūṇṭa talimakkaḷil tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam peṟṟa talimakaṉ varintukōṭaṟkaṇ viriyātu kātal māṇpuṟuvatu karuti nīṭṭit toḻukukiṟāṉ. avaḷatu kātal peruki avaṉi iṉṟiyamiyā aḷaviṉi yaṭintatu kaṇṭa tōḻi,  avaṉik kuṟippāl varivu kaṭāvalāṉāḷ. orukāl talimakaṉ pōntu talivi maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟattē vantuniṟpatu kaṇṭu taliviyōṭu  collāṭupavaḷ pōṉṟu avaṉ cevippaṭumāṟu oru putar arukē niṉṟu, niṉṉi uṇpikkak koṇarnta pāl poṉvaḷḷattil kaṭṭiliṉ kīḻiruppa atu niṉmēṉi pōlum poṉṉiṟam peṟṟatu; nīyum kaṭṭiliṉ iṟaṅkic ciṟitum naṭakkiṉ ṟāyilli; paṭukkiyi yum  veṟuttāy pōla eḻuntu uṭkārntu kaḷḷuṇṭu makiḻntāṟ pōlak kaṇ civantuḷḷaṉi; ivvēṟupāṭu eṉṉiyeṉavum nī niṉittili; niṉ uḷḷattuk kuṟippum mikap peritāka uḷatu. talivaṉākiya kātalaṉ pōntu niṉ nalla uḷḷattik kavarntu koṇṭami yuṇarāmiyiṉ, aṉṉi yum miyalu ṟāḷāyiṉum piṟar aiyuṟāvāṟu kaṭuṅkuraleṭuttu aḻikkiṉṟāḷ; kātalarō nam nili yuṇarntu varikiṉṟā rilli; yāṉ ceyvataṟiyātu varuntukiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟu coṉ ṉāḷ. 
tōḻiyiṉatu ivvuriyiṉkaṇ, taliviyiṉ kātaṟ ciṟap pum tāyaṟiviṉ nuṭpamum vēṟṟuvarivuk kuṟippum aṭaṅki niṟkum nayam kaṇṭa maḷḷaṉār avaṟṟi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.  
poṉcey vaḷḷattup pālkiḻak kiruppa
niṉṉoḷi ēṟiya cēvaṭi otuṅkāy
paṉmāṇ cēkki 1pakikoḷa niṉii
makiḻā nōkkam makiḻntaṉi2 pōṉṟaṉi
evaṉkol eṉṟu niṉikkalum3 niṉittili
niṉṉuḷ tōṉṟum kuṟippunaṉi peritē
4cetunaṉi muṇiiya citarkāl vāraṇam
5mutirkaṟi yāppil tuñcum nāṭaṉ
mella vantuniṉ nallakam peṟṟami
6miya luṟāaḷ aṉṉi
aiya miṉṟik kaṭuṅkū viṉaḷē.
itu, tōḻi ciṟippuṟamākat talimakaṭku urippāḷāyt talimakaṉ kēṭpac colliyatu; tōḻi talimakaḷi aṟattoṭu nili valippittatūumām. 
uri : 
poṉcey vaḷḷattup pāl kiḻakkiruppa- poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kiṇṇattiṟ peytu kīḻē vittirukkum pāl; niṉ oḷi ēṟiya - niṉ mēṉiyiṉ poṉṉoḷi koṇṭaṉa; cēvaṭi otuṅkāy - civanta niṉ aṭiyeṭuttu nī naṭakkiṉṟā yilli; paṉmāṇ cēkki pakikoḷa niṉii-palavāy māṭcimippaṭṭa paṭukkiyum vemmiyuṟṟup paki cey talāl yātō niṉintu; makiḻā nōkkam makiḻntaṉi pōṉṟaṉi - kaḷḷuṇṭāreytum miyal nōkkiṟku iṭamiṉṟākavum ataṉi uṇṭāṟ pōlum nōkkamuṭi yāyiṉi; evaṉ kol eṉṟu niṉikkalum niṉittili - eṉṉi kāraṇameṉavum nī niṉikkiṉṟāyilli; niṉṉuḷ tōṉṟum kuṟippum naṉiperitu - niṉpāl tōṉṟum kuṟippukkaḷum mikap periyavākum; cetunaṉi muṇiiya citarkāl vāraṇam - vāṭiya arumpukaḷi mēyntu veṟutta citarnta iṟaku poruntiya kālkaḷiyuṭiya kōḻi; mutir kaṟi yāppil tuñcum nāṭaṉ - mutirnta miḷakukkoṭikku vakutta pāttiyil kiṭantu uṟaṅkum nāṭaṉākiya talivaṉ; mella vantu - piyap pōntu; niṉ nallakam peṟṟami - niṉ nallavuḷḷattik kavarntu koṇṭami yaṟiyāmiyiṉ; miyal uṟāaḷ aṉṉi - aṟintuḻi yeytum mayakkatti uṟāḷākiya aṉṉi; aiyam iṉṟik kaṭuṅ kūviṉaḷ - aiyak kuṟippoṉṟum tōṉṟā vakiyil mikka kuraleṭuttu aḻiyāniṉṟaṉaḷkāṇ e.ṟu. 
pāl iruppa ēṟiya; cēvaṭi otuṅkāy; pakikoḷa niṉii makiḻntaṉi pōṉṟaṉi, niṉikkalum niṉittili; kuṟippu naṉi peritu; peṟṟami miyaluṟāaḷ aṉṉi kaṭuṅkūviṉaḷ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. pāl ney mutaliya nīriyaṟ poruḷkaḷip paṉmiyāṟ kūṟum vaḻakkup paṟṟip pāl kiḻakkiruppa eṉṟār. kiḻakku, kīḻ. naṉku kāynta pāl niṟañ civantu ēṭupaṭiyak kuḷirntavaḻi viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum iyal piṟṟu; atu poṉvaḷḷattiṟ peytu vittavaḻic cenniṟam mikutaliṉ, oḷiēṟiya eṉṟār. otuṅkutal, naṭattal. pañcu, pīli mutaliya palvaki meṉporuḷāl aminta paṭukki yātaliṉ paṉmāṇ cēkki eṉappaṭṭatu. paṭukkiyum vemmiyuṟṟu iruttaṟ kākāvāṟu māṟutalāl pakikoḷa eṉṟār. ceya veṉeccam kāraṇapporuṭṭu. kaḷḷuṇṭalāṟ piṟakkum miyal makiḻtal eṉappaṭum; “makiḻntataṉ taliyum naṟavuṇ ṭāṅku1” eṉpatu kāṇka. makiḻcciyāl tōṉṟum miyal nōkkam makiḻnta nōkkam eṉṟum, miyaliṉṟit teḷinta pārvi makiḻā nōkkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. “uṇṇalum uṇṇēṉ”1 eṉṟāṟ pōla niṉikkalum niṉittili eṉavantatu. cetunaṉi “cetumoḻi”2 cetukkaṇ3 eṉṟāṟ pōlap payaṉillāmi cuṭṭi niṉṟatu. ciṟakukaḷ ciṟukic cetiḷ palkic citaṟiṉāṟ pōlat tōṉṟutaliṉ kōḻiyiṉ kāl citarkāl eṉappaṭṭatu. yāppu, pātti, “yāppiṉuḷ aṭṭiya nīr”4 eṉpatu kāṇka. kaṟikkoṭi mutirnta viṭattu nīr corital vēṇṭāmiyiṉ, ataṉkaṇ vāraṇam tuñcuvatāyiṟṟu eṉka. nallakam, nalla  maṉatteḻum kātalaṉpu. miyal, īṇṭu vekuḷi mēṟṟu, kaṭuṅkū, mikka kūppīṭu; iriccalumām. 
ciṟippuṟattē niṉṟa talimakaṉ cevippaṭumāṟu tōḻi taliviyoṭu collāṭuvāḷ, avaḷ vēṭki mikku  uṇṭi veṟuttu niṟam vēṟupaṭṭami pulappaṭa, poṉcey vaḷḷattup pāl kiḻakkiruppa niṉ oḷi ēṟiya eṉṟum, ataṉāl uṭampunaṉi curuṅkiṉamiyiṉ naṭattal melintaṉi eṉṟaṟkuc cēvaṭi yotuṅkāy eṉṟum, neṭi tiruttaliṉ paṭukkiyum veytuṟṟu vemmi ceytaliṉ irukkamāṭṭātu maruṇṭu nōkkukiṉṟaṉi eṉpāḷ, paṉmāṇ cēkki pakikoḷa niṉii makiḻā nōkkamoṭu makiḻntaṉi pōṉṟaṉi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. kūṭṭamiṉmiyiṉ makiḻā nōkkatti vitantōtik kātal kimmikal tōṉṟa makiḻnta nōkkattik kūṟiṉāḷ, ivvēṟupāṭṭāl payaṉ yātu milli; kātalar teruṇṭuvaritali niṉittilar eṉpāḷ, evaṉkol eṉṟu niṉikkalum niṉittili yeṉṟum, tāyaṟintu  iṟceṟippu mikuvippiṉum talimakaṉik kāṇṭal aritāyiṉum uṭaṉpōkku mutaliyaṉa mēṟkōṭaṟku ēṟṟa vaṉkaṇmik kuṟippum niṉpāl viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṟateṉ pāḷ, niṉṉuḷ tōṉṟum kuṟippu naṉiperitu eṉṟum, uḷḷuṟiyāl vēṟṟōr makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭi vantu iruppati yuṇarttutaliṉ niṉakkum talimakaṟku muḷatākiya kātaluṟavi yaṟiyāmiyiṉ aṉṉi vekuḷimayakkamiṉṟi vēṟoṉṟu niṉittoḻukukiṉṟāḷ eṉpāḷ miyal uṟāaḷ aṉṉi yeṉṟum, eṉiṉum, avaṭku añci avaḷ āṇivaḻiyoḻukum niṉ aruñceyal māṇpi ayalavar aṟiyavum, niṉ kaḷavu mēlum nīṭṭiyavāṟu toṭarpaṟumāṟum, pērāravāram ceytoḻukukiṉṟāḷ eṉṟum, eṉavē tali makaṉ iṉiyum teruḷāṉāyiṉ vēṟṟavar varivum ētam palavum eytumeṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. cetunaṉi muṇiiya citarkāl vāraṇam mutirkaṟi yāppil tuñcum eṉṟatu, piṟavayiṉ makaṭ kōṭal veṟuttup pōnta notumalar atu viḻintu vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu uḷḷuṟuttavāṟu, talimakaṉ teruṇṭu varivāṉāvatu payaṉ.

298.viṟṟūṟṟu vaṇṇakkaṉ tattaṉār
palakkaṉūṟṟu, tāḻiyūṟṟu eṉṟāṟpōla viṟṟūṟṟu eṉpatu ōr ūr; itu pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉkaṇ iruntu maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. tattaṉār eṉṟa iccāṉṟōr ivvūrkkaṇ vaṇṇakkar toḻil purintu vantavar. ataṉāl ivar viṟṟūṟṟu vaṇṇakkaṉ tattaṉār eṉṟa  peyar peṟuvārāyiṉār. ivvūr nallicic cāṉṟōrāṉa mūteyiṉaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōrkkum uriyatākum. ākavē, ivvūr cāṉṟōr tōṉṟum cāl puṭiyateṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkum. ittattaṉār pāṇṭiyaṉatu kūṭalnakarip pārāṭṭutalāl ivarkku annakar pāluḷḷa īṭupāṭu naṉku terikiṟatu. ivar pāṭiyatāka vēṟu pāṭṭukkaḷ kiṭittila. 
vatuvi maṇam puṇarntu maṉiyaṟam mēṟkoṇṭa talimakkaḷ vāḻvil, talivaṉ poruḷ ceyyak karutit taliviyiṟ pirintu cella vēṇṭiya kaṭamiyuṭi yaṉāṉāṉ. poruḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyum kātaliyiṉ pirivarumiyum avaṉ uḷḷatti alittaṉa. ivviru karuttukkaḷum tammuṉ nēr niṉṟu pōrāṭutaṟku avaṉmaṉam nilikkaḷamāyiṟṟē yaṉṟi orutaliyākat tuṇitaṟkut tuṇiceyyavilli. ataṉāl kalaṅkañar eytik kiyaṟṟa avaṉ uḷḷattil, kātalvaḻi niṉṟa oru karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu; talimakaḷi mutaṟkaṇ tāṉ talippeyta ñāṉṟu, “piriyēṉ; piriyiṉ uyir tariyēṉ” eṉṟu moḻinta orumiyuriyiyum ataṉi maṉaṅ koṇṭu makiḻntu nōkkiya taliviyiṉ kātaṟpārvi yiyum atu niṉippittatu. nam piriviṉkaṇ iṭiyiṟ kaṭantu cellum perumalikaḷi nōkkik kaṇ kaliḻum kātaliyiṉ avalap pārviyip peṟutal kātal vāḻvukkup poruntuva taṉṟu eṉpatu talivaṉ neñciṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya putukkaruttu. ataṉi yavaṉ talivi kēṭkumāṟu taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟuvāṉ pōl eṭutturittāṉ. atu kēṭṭu avaḷum oruvāṟu teḷintu tēṟiṉāḷ. 
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talimakaṉ tāṉ uritta moḻi yorumiyiyum kātaliyiṉ pirivarumiyiyum eṭuttōtip pirivu viḻiyātāṉ pōla urittu uḷḷuṟiyāl piriyiṉum uḷḷam piriyātu peyarntu pōntu talimakaḷik kūṭum tiṟam kūṟic celavaḻuṅkum nayam kaṇṭa nam vaṇṇakkaṉ tattaṉār atu poruḷāka vittu ippāṭṭip pāṭukiṉṟār. 
vampa mākkaḷ varutiṟam nōkkic
ceṅkaṇi toṭutta ceyirnōk kāṭavar
maṭivāyt taṇṇumi taḻaṅkukural kēṭṭa
eruvic cēval 1 kiḷivayiṟ peyarum 
aruñcurak kavali añcuvaru naṉantalip 
perumpal kuṉṟam uḷḷiyum maṟṟivaḷ
karumpuṭip paṇittōḷ nōkkiyum orutiṟam
paṟṟāy vāḻiem neñcē naṟṟārp 
poṟṟērc ceḻiyaṉ2 kūṭal aṉṉa
orumi ceppiya arumi vāṉmuki
irumpōtu kamaḻum kūntal
3perumali taḻīiyanōk 4kiyiyumō maṟṟē 
itu, poruḷ valippittut talimakaḷi eyti āṟṟātāya neñciṟku neruṅkic collit talimakaṉ celavaḻuṅkiyatu. 
uri : 
vampamākkaḷ varutiṟam nōkki - putuvōrāy varukiṉṟa makkaḷuṭiya amitiyip pārttu; ceṅkaṇi toṭutta ceyir nōkku āṭavar - cevviya ampu toṭuttu varuttum ciṉanta  pārviyiyuṭiya maṟavar muḻakkum; maṭivāyt taṇṇumi taḻaṅkukural kēṭṭa eruvic cēval - tōl maṭikkappaṭṭa vāyiyuṭiya taṇṇumiyiṉ muḻakkattik kēṭṭa paruntiṉ āṇpaṟavi; kiḷivayiṉ peyarum - taṉ kiḷiyākiya peṭiyum piṟavum vāḻum iṭam nōkkic cellum; aruñcurak kavali añcuvaru naṉantali - kaṭattaṟ kariya curattiluḷḷa kavartta vaḻikaḷāl celvōrkku accam tarum akaṉṟa iṭattē niṟkum; perumpal kuṉṟam uḷḷiyum - periya palavākiya kuṉṟaṅkaḷi niṉintum; ivaḷ karum puṭip paṇittōḷ nōkkiyum - ivaḷuṭiya karum peḻutiya periya tōḷip pārttum; oru tiṟam paṟṟāy -oru pālum tuṇiyātu mayaṅkukiṉṟāy; em neñcē -; vāḻi -; naṟṟār poṟṟērc ceḻiyaṉ  kūṭal aṉṉa - nalla vēmpiṉ māliyaṇinta poṟṟērc ceḻiyaṉ eṉpā ṉuṭiya kūṭalnakar pōṉṟa; orumi ceppiya arumi - paṇṭu yām orutaliyāyt teḷiya vuritta ariya moḻi yiṉi niṉintu; vāṉmuki irumpōtu kamaḻum kūntal - veṇmiyāṉa arumpukaḷ periya pūkkaḷāy malarntu maṇam kamaḻum kūntaliyuṭiya namtalivi ; perumali taḻīiya nōkku iyiyumō - nām kaṭantu  ceṉṟa perumali kaḷi  nōkkik kaliḻum kaṭpārviyi nām kāṇṭal kātaluṟavukku amiyumō?  kūṟuka eṉṟu. 
neñcē. uḷḷiyum nōkkiyum paṟṟāyāyiṉi; kūṭalaṉṉa orumi ceppiya arumi  niṉintu kūntal taḻīiya nōkkiṉi yum poruḷ ceyyumiṭattup peṟal amiyumō, kūṟuka eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. vampamākkaḷ, putiyarāy varuvōr. piṟivāyk kaṇiyiṉ nīkkutaṟkuc ceṅkaṇi eṉṟār. kurutik kaṟi paṭintamiyāṟ civanta ampu eṉṟumām. vekuḷaṟ kuriya kāraṇamiṉṟākavum vekuṇṭārpōṟ civanta kaṇkaḷi yuṭimiyiṉ, ceyirnōk kāṭavar eṉṟār. āṭavar, pōr maṟavar eṉṉum poruṭṭu. vāy kūritāyavaḻit taṇṇumiyil pōppuṟum tōl eḷitiṟ kiḻintoḻiyumātaliṉ taṇṇu mikku maṭivāy vēṇṭappaṭṭatu.  taḻaṅkukural , viṉit toki; itu taḻaṅkural eṉavum varum. eruvi, paruntu. kavali, kavartta vaḻi. karumpuṭittōḷ, paṇittōḷ eṉa iyiyum. makaḷir tōḷkaḷil karumpu pōla eḻutuvatu paṇṭiyōr marapu; itaṉit toyyil eḻututal eṉṟalu muṇṭu. paṟṟutal, īṇṭut tuṇital mēṟṟu. peṟṟōrc ceḻiyaṉ, paṇṭi nāḷip pāṇṭimaṉṉaruḷ oruvaṉ; vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum tērmāṟaṉ eṉ pāṉ pōlpavaṉ. kūṭal, maturi nakariṉ mēlippakuti, maturikkum viyik karikkum iṭip pakuti ālavāyil. orumi, uṟutimoḻi. muki, arumpu , pōtu, malarum,   cevvippū. “kāli arumpip pakalellām pōtāki, māli malarum” innōy1”eṉpatu kāṇka. kūntal , kūntaliyuṭiya talimakaḷic cuṭṭiṟṟu. 
pirital vēṇṭiya kaṭappāṭṭiṉi mēṟkoṇṭa tali makaṉ talimakaḷi ataṟku uṭampaṭuttal virumpit taṉ neñcōṭu kūṟuvāṉ pōla avaṭkuk kūṟaluṟṟu, mutaṟkaṇ tāṉ cellum curattiṉ arumiyi yeṇṇic celavu taviāppāṉāy, “neñcē, nī curattiṉkaṇ vāḻum maṟavaratu koṭumiyum avarāl viḷiyum ētamum niṉintu aḻuṅku kiṉṟaṉi” yeṉpāṉ, ceyir nōkkāṭavar maṭivāyt taṇṇumit taḻaṅku kural kēṭṭa eruvic cēval kiḷivayiṟ peyarum perumpal kuṉṟam uḷḷiyum eṉṟāṉ.curattiṉkaṭ pōntu payiṉṟa paḻiyōrāyiṉ aṅku vāḻum maṟavariṉum mikka valiyuṭaṉ varuvarātaliṉ vampa mākkaḷ varutiṟam nōkki eṉṟum, nērniṟṟaliṉṟi maṟin tiruntu celuttutaliṉ ceṅkaṇi toṭutta āṭavar  eṉṟum, aṟam nōkkātu nērpaṭṭār yāvariyumaruḷiṉṟik kollum vaṉkaṇṇar eṉṟaṟkuc  ceyir nōkku āṭavar eṉṟum, avaruṭiya taṇṇumi muḻakkam kēṭpiṉ vāṉiṟ paṟakkum eruvic cēvalum añcit taṉ kiḷippuṭkaḷ vāḻum iṭanāṭic cellum eṉṟataṉāl maṇmēṟ cellum makkaḷ collaṟkariya tuṇukkuṟṟu alamaruvar eṉṟum, eṉavē avar uṟiyum kuṉṟaṅkaḷik kaṭantu cēṟaliṉ arumi niṉikku māṟu tōṉṟa aruñcurak kavali añcuvaru naṉattalip perumpal kuṉṟam uḷḷiyum eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. oru pāl talimakaḷatu tōṭ perumiyum ataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum muyak kiṉpamum tōṉṟip piriviṉpāṟ cellum uḷḷattip pētuṟuvittu avaḷkūṭṭattoṭu piṇikkiṉṟamiyiṉ ivaḷ karumpuṭip paṇittōḷ nōkkiyum eṉṟum, iv vāṟṟāl pirivu piriyāmi eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ yātēṉum oṉṟi orutaliyāt tuṇiyāmi paṟṟi, orutiṟam paṟṟāy em neñcē eṉṟum, itaṉāl nī ikaḻaṟpāliyalli eṉpāṉ vāḻi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. viṉi āṭavarkku uyi rātalpaṟṟip pirivē paṟṟiṉiyāyiṉ, paṇṭu kaḷaviṉkaṇ mutaṉmutal talippeytapōtu “piriyōṉ piriyiṉ uyir tariyēṉ” eṉṟa taṉ orumimoḻiyit talivi niṉintu varuntuvaḷ eṉpāṉ orumi ceppiya arumi niṉintu  eṉṟāṉ. niṉintu eṉpatu colleccam. pirintavaḻit tāṉ kaṭantu ceṉṟa perumalikaḷi nōkkit taṉṉik kāṇa māṭṭā miyiṉ talivi varuntik kaṇṇīr corintu varuntu vaḷ eṉavum, kātalvāḻviṟku atu poruntāteṉavum, eṉavē pirital kūṭāteṉavum kūṟuvāṉ, perumali taḻīiya nōkku iyiyumō maṟṟē eṉṟu celavaḻuṅkiṉāṉ, ceyir nōkku āṭavaratu taṇṇumi muḻakkam kēṭṭa eruvic cēval kiḷivayiṟ peyarum eṉṟatu, piriviṉkaṇ kātaliyiṉ kaṇ kaluḻntu ceyyum pūcal eṉakkut teriyiṉ eṉ uḷḷam uṭaṉē ceyviṉiyik kiyikantu avaḷpāṟ ceṉṟoḻiyum eṉpatu uḷḷuṟutturittavāṟu. talivi kēṭṭuc cela vuṭaṉpaṭu vāḷāvatu payaṉ.

299. vaṭama vaṇṇakkaṉ pēricāttaṉār
kaḷavu neṟikkaṇ tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam peṟumvakiyil kuṟi maṟukkappaṭṭum cēṭpaṭukkappaṭṭum peruntuya ruḻakkum talimakaṉ atu peṟṟa piṉ taliviyuḷḷattuk kātal māṇ puṟutaṟ poruṭṭu akkaḷavu oḻukkatti nīṭṭippaṉ. pakaṟ kuṟikkaṇ pōtaruvōṉi maṟiveḷippaṭutaṟkañcit tōḻi iravukkuṟikkaṇ varumāṟu tūṇṭuvaḷ. kāvaṉmikutiyālum allakuṟiyālum piṟavāṟṟālum iravukkuṟiyoḻukkam iṭi yīṭupaṭum; iravu varavil neṟiyiṉ koṭumiyum kārmaḻi mikutiyum kāṭṭāṟukaḷiṉ kaṭuvaravum koṭuvilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyakkamum kāvalarumiyum ākiya iṉṉa piṟa iṭiyūṟukaḷi veḷippaṭiyākavum kuṟippākavum tōḻi talimakaṟ kurittu iravu varuvōṉip pakal varuka eṉpaḷ. iravumpakalum payiṉṟu varuvāṉāyiṉ irupōtum vāraṟka eṉa maṟuppāḷ, ivvaṇṇam varuntutali nīkki ivaḷi varintukoḷka eṉpāḷ. ivvāṟu tōḻi varivu kaṭāviyavaḻiyum, talimakaṉ ataṉi mēṟkoḷḷātu pakaṟ pōtukaḷil talivimaṉiyiṉ oru ciṟikkaṭ pōntu niṉṟu taṉ kātaṟ perukkik kuṟippāl uṇarttuvaṉ. akkālattē tōḻiyō talimakaḷō ava ṉukku nērē uriyāṭāmal piṟar kēṭpiṉ ayirāta coṟ kaḷāl kūṭutalarumiyuṇartti varintu koḷḷumāṟu avaṉik kaṭāvuvar. orunāḷ talimakaṉ pōntu ciṟippuṟattē niṟpatu tōḻikkut terintatu. tōḻi talimakaḷi appakkattē koṇarntu niṟuttit tāṉ kūṟuvaṉa avaṉ cevippaṭumāṟu uriyāṭaluṟṟu, “tōḻi, talimakaṟkum namakkum uḷatākiya toṭarpu eṅṅaṉamō ivvūravarkkut terintu viṭṭatu. nām taṉittirup pakkāṇiṉ avaratu alar peritāyuḷatu; tali makaṉoṭu ittoṭarpi nām koḷḷāmuṉpu ivvūr nammit tūṟṟiyatumilli; alarurikku nām poruḷāṉatu milli” eṉṟāḷ. 
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, ūril alareḻuntamiyum talimakaṉ teruṇṭu varintu aruḷāta koṭumiyum kūṟi varivu mēṟkoḷḷumāṟu tōḻi kaṭāviya nayam kaṇṭa pēri cātta ṉār ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ataṉi amittup pāṭukiṉṟār. 
1urukē ḻiyāṉik 4kuṭikoṇ ṭaṉṉa 
tatarpiṇi aviḻnta tāḻi vāṉputar 
tayaṅkiruṅ kōṭi tākkaliṉ nuṇṭātu 
vayaṅkiḻi makaḷir 2vaḷiyiṉ tāam 
kāmar ciṟukuṭip pulampiṉum3 alarkāṇ 
nāmila mākutal aṟitum maṉṉō
villeṟi pañci pōla malkutiri
vaḷiporu vayaṅkupicir poṅkum 
naḷikaṭaṟ cērppaṉoṭu nakāa ūṅkē.
itu, talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi colliyatu. 
uri : 
urukēḻ yāṉik kuṭikoṇṭaṉṉa - naṉṉiṟam poruntiya yāṉi taṉmēl kuṭiviriyak koṇṭu niṉ ṟāṟpōla; tatarpiṇi aviḻnta tāḻi vāṉputar - neruṅkiya itaḻppiṇippu viriya malarntu viḷaṅkum tāḻiyiṉ periya putar; tayaṅku iruṅkōṭi tākkaliṉ - acikiṉṟa periya kōṭikkāṟṟu mōtutalāl; nuṇṭātu - nuṇṇiya veṇpoṭiyāṉatu; vayaṅkiḻi makaḷir vaḷiyiṉ tāam - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa iḻiyaṇinta makaḷiruṭiya kivaḷi pōla utirum; kāmar ciṟu kuṭi pulampiṉum - aḻakiya iccīṟūrkkaṇ yām taṉimiyuṟṟu varuntiya viṭattum; alar nām ila mākutal aṟitum kāṇ - alar kūṟappaṭutali nām illē māy iruntami nām naṉku aṟivōm; vil eṟi pañcipōla - villiṭattu eṟiyappaṭṭa pañcittuy pōla; malku tiri vaḷiporu vayaṅkupicir poṅkum - aṭuttaṭuttu varum alikaḷik kāṟṟup porutu alittalāl ōḷirum nīrttivalikaḷ poṅki yeḻukiṉṟa; naḷikaṭaṟ cērppa ṉoṭu nakāa vūṅku - periya kaṭalnilat talivaṉoṭu kūṭi makiḻvataṟku muṉpu e.ṟu. 
cērppaṉoṭu nakāa vūṅkuc ciṟukuṭip pulampiṉum nām alar ilamākutal aṟitum eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. kāṇ, muṉṉili yaci. maṉṉum ōvum acinili. kēḻ cāriyi; keḻu veṉṉum niṟap poruṭṭāya uriccolliṉ tiripeṉak koḷḷiṉumām. tatarpiṇi, neruṅkiya itaḻp piṇippu, kōṭi, kōṭik kāṟṟu. tāḻip pūviṉ aṭip pakuti makaḷir aṇiyum vaḷipōṟaliṉ vaḷiyiṉ tāam eṉṟār; iṉ oppup poruṭṭu, pulampu, taṉimi. aṟital, niṉiviṟ kōṭal. koṭṭiyum kōtu māyavaṟṟiṉ nīkku taṟkup payaṉpaṭum karuvi vilpōṟaliṉ villeṉappaṭṭatu. alikaḷ picirpicirāka vuṭintu villiṭi yeṟiyappaṭṭup poṅkum pañci pōṟaliṉ villeṟi pañci pōla eṉṟār.   “villeṟi pañciyiṉ veṇmaḻi tavaḻum1 eṉap piṟarum kūṟupa.  naḷi, perumi. nakāa vūṅku,nakutaṟku muṉpu: vāṉṟōy veṟpaṉ maṇavā vūṅku”2 eṉṟāṟ pōla. 
ciṟip puṟattu niṉṟa talimakaṉ kēṭka uriyāṭu kiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, avaṟku ūralar urittu varivu kaṭāvaluṟum tōḻi alarkkuriya kāraṇatti uḷḷuṟiyāṟ kūṟaliṉ, alaruṇmiyi etirmaṟi vāypāṭṭāl cērppaṉoṭu nakāa vūṅkuc ciṟukuṭip pulampiṉum alar nām ila mākutal aṟitum maṉṉō eṉṟāḷ. tāḻi maṭal  virintu niṟpaṉa yāṉi kuṭi koṇṭāṟ pōlat  tōṉṟiṉum, utirkiṉṟa itaḻkaḷ makaḷir vaḷi utirntāṟ pōlum eṉṟatu, talimakaṉatu oḻukkam namakkup perumi tantu aruḷuvatu pōlat tōṉṟiṉum avaṉuṭiya pōkkuvaravu namakku vaḷi nekiḻumāṟu uṭampu naṉi curuṅkum nōy ceytaṉa. ataṉāl alar eḻuvatu eḷitāyiṟ ṟeṉṟāḷām. vaḷi porutalāl eḻum picir villeṟiyap paṭṭa pañcu pōlap poṅkip paravum eṉṟatu, vēṭki nōy alittalāl uḷatāya vēṟupāṭu paṟṟiya ūr muḻutum paravi viṭṭatu eṉa uḷḷuṟuttavāṟu. itaṉāṟ payaṉ talivaṉ teruṇṭu varivāṉāvatu.
300.paraṇar
kaṟpukkaṭampūṇṭu poṟpuṭi aṟampurintoḻukum talimakkaḷ vāḻvil, talimakaḷ taliccūṟkoṇṭu makaṟ payantu uṭaṉuṟi vāḻkkikku ollāḷāya kālattut tali makaṟkup parattimi poruntiya puṟattoḻukkam uṇ ṭāyiṟṟu. parattiyar cūḻalil avaṉ oḻukiya ñāṉṟu talik koṇṭoḻukiya paratti piṟa parattiyariṉ pirintoḻuku kiṉṟāṉeṉa avaṟku vāyiṉ maṟuttāḷ. tāṉ viṭutta vāyil kaḷāl apparattiyiṉ ciṉam taṇiyāmi kaṇṭa talimakaṉ taṉṉoṭu pōnta pāṇaṉiyum tēriyum avaḷ maṉik kaṇṇē niṟuttic ceṉṟāṉ. pāṇaṉ avaḷ vāyil vēṇṭi maṉippuṟattu aṭṭiṟkāliyiṉ arukē ceṉṟu niṉṟāṉ. avaṉ varavuṇarnta paratti avaṉi nōkki varalum, mukam kāṇa nāṇiṉāṉ pōla aṭṭiliṉ kūriyākiya paṉi yōliyi nimirttu ataṉip paṟṟikkoṇṭu niṉṟāṉ. avaṟku apparatti vāyil maṟukkum karuttiṉaḷāy, “pāṇa, niṉ talivaṉākiya ūraṉ, eṉ kiyiluḷḷa ciṟuvaḷikku viliyākap periyatoru tēri oppaṉi ceytu koṇarntu eṉ maṉimuṉṟilil niṟuttiviṭṭu vēṟu paratti maṉikkuc ceṉṟoḻintāṉ. avaṉ tēroṭu pōnta nīyum avaṉ piṉṉē cellātu taṉ uṭalic cūḻap piccamaṇintu niṟkum peruṅkaḷiṟu pōla aṭṭiṟ kūriyiṉ paṉiyōliyit toṭuttuk koṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉi; aṅṅaṉē niṟpāyāka” eṉṟāḷ. 
parattiyiṉ ikkūṟṟu, talivaṉatu parattimiyic ciṟappittup pāṇaṉi vāyil maṟutta vāypāṭṭāl tali makaṉit tarumāṟu paṇikkum nuṭpam kaṇṭu viyanta āciriyar paraṇar ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.  
cuṭarttoṭik kōmakaḷ ciṉanteṉa ataṉetir maṭattaki āyam kitoḻu tāaṅ

aṭṭil ōli toṭṭaṉi niṉmē.
itu, parattit talivi pāṇaṟku vāyiṉ maṟuttatu; varivu kaṭāyatuumām, māṟṟōr notumalāḷar variviṉ mēliṭṭu marutattuk kaḷavu. 
uri : 
cuṭarttoṭik kōmakaḷ ciṉanteṉa - oḷirkiṉṟa toṭiyaṇinta aracamātēvi ciṉantu koṇṭāḷāka;  ataṉ etir - ataṟku añci niṟkum; maṭattaki āyam kitoḻu tāaṅku - iḷampeṇkaḷākiya ēvalmakaḷir kūṭṭam kikūppit toḻutāṟ pōla; uṟukāl oṟṟa olki - mikka kāṟṟu acittalāl taḷarntu; āmpal tāmarikku iṟiñcum - āmpaṟ pūkkaḷ kuvintu, malarntu viḷaṅkum tāmariyiṉ etiril cāyntoḻukum; taṇtuṟi ūraṉ - taṇṇiya tuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya ūraṉākiya talimakaṉ; ciṟu vaḷi viliyeṉa - muṉkic ciṟuvaḷikaṭku viliyāka; peruntēr paṇṇi - periya tēri oppaṉi ceytu; em muṉkaṭi niṟīic ceṉṟiciṉōṉ - emmuṭiya illiṉ muṉ niṟuttiviṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉaṉ; nīyum tēroṭu vantu pōtal cellātu - nīyum attērōṭu pōntu ava ṉōṭē ceṉṟoḻiyāmal;neyvārntaṉṉa tuyyaṭaṅku narampiṉ  - neyyoḻukiṉāṟ  pōṉṟa tuyyākiya picir pōkki icikku aṭaṅka amitta narampiṉiyuṭiya yāḻi icikkum; irumpāṇ okkal talivaṉ - periya pāṇ cuṟṟattārkkut talivaṉum; perumpūṇ ēer  taḻum paṉ  ūṇūr āṅkaṇ - periya pūṇāra maṇinta ēṟṟatti yuṭiya taḻumpaṉ eṉpāṉuṭiya ūṇūr eṉṉum ūriṉ kaṇ; picci cūḻ peruṅkaḷiṟu pōla; uṭampu cūḻvarap pakkam aṇintu niṟkum periya kaḷiṟṟip pōla em aṭṭil ōli toṭṭaṉi - em maṉiyiṉ aṭṭiṟ kūriyiṉ oliyit tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉi; niṉmē - aṅṅaṉē niṟpāyāka e.ṟu. 
ūraṉ, ciṟuvaḷi viliyeṉat tērpaṇṇi niṟīic ceṉṟiciṉēṉ; nīyum tēroṭu vantu pōtal cellātu kaḷiṟu pōla aṭṭil ōli toṭṭaṉi, niṉmē eṉakkūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. cuṭart toṭi, oḷi poruntiya toṭi. ataṉ etir eṉpuḻi atu, ciṉattiṉ mēl niṉṟatu. maṭattaki āyam, iḷamakaḷir kūṭṭam. maṭattaki eṉpuḻi maṭam iḷami yum taki aḻakum kuṟittaṉaveṉiṉum amiyum. kitoḻu tal, kikūppit toḻutal. iravil malarum āmpal, tāmari malarntu viḷaṅkum pakaṟpōtil, itaḻ kuvintu tuvaṇṭu tāḻtaṟkuk kōmakaḷ ciṉattiṉ muṉ āyamakaḷir kitoḻutu tāḻtal uvamamāyiṟṟu. parattiyarkku nalkappaṭum paricam vaḷivili eṉavum viṟaliyarkku nalkuvatu pūviliyeṉavum vaḻaṅkum. paṇṇutal, ceppamum oppaṉiyum amiyac ceytal, ceṉṟiciṉōṉ: iciṉ paṭarkkikkaṇ vantatu, ā ōvāyiṟṟu, ceyyuḷātaliṉ. narampu , ākupeyar. ēer, eḻucci. ūṇur, pāṇṭināṭṭu maruṅkūrp paṭṭiṉattukku aṇimiyil irunta ūr. “ūṇūrumpar viḻuniti tuñcum, vīṟupeṟu tirunakar iruṅkaḻip paṭappi maruṅkūrp paṭṭiṉattu”1 eṉṟum, “mīṉ cīvum pāṇcēri vāymoḻit taḻumpaṉ ūṇūr”2 eṉṟum taḻumpaṉ pārāṭṭappaṭuvaṉ. piccam, ammuk keṭṭu aikāram peṟṟup picci eṉa niṉṟatu. pikkam - mayiṟpīliyāl ceyyap paṭuvatu.  picci cūḻ peruṅkaḷiṟu  - piccaṅkaḷ cūḻa niṉṟa kaḷiṟu. piccaṅkaḷiṉ kūṭṭam paṉimaraṅkaḷiṉ kūṭṭam pōlakkāṭci tarutaliṉ, paṉiyōlikaḷiṉ pakkal niṉṟa pāṇaṟku aḵtu uvamamāyiṟṟu. “maṭaṟpaṉik kuḻāttiṟ piccam niṟintaṉa”3 eṉat tēvarum eṭuttōtutal kāṇka. “muṉṉili muṉṉar īyu mēyum, annili marapiṉ meyyūrntu varumē”4 eṉpataṉāl niṉmē eṉa muṉṉili ēkāram makara meyyūrntu niṉṟatu. 
talimakaḷ poruṭṭu vāyil vēṇṭivanta pāṇaṟku mutaṟkaṇ paratti vāyil maṟutturikkiṉṟāḷākaliṉ taṇṭuṟiyūraṉ peruntēr paṇṇi em muṉkaṭi niṟīic ceṉṟiciṉōṉ eṉṟum , eṉavē avaṉporuṭṭu vāyil vēṇṭum karuttoṭu vantili; avaṉ tērōṭu pōntu atuniṟpa niṉṟaṉiyaṉṟi vēṟilli eṉap pāṇaṉi maṟup pāḷ, nīyum tērōṭuvantu pōtal cellātu em aṭṭil ōli toṭṭaṉi eṉṟāḷ. eṉṟatu, tēriṉvaḻi niṟ kiṉṟaṉiyēyaṉṟi, tēr tanta avaṉ vaḻi niṉṟu avaṉ cel vaḻic ceṉṟu avaṉik koṇarāyāyiṉi yātaliṉ, em maṉikkuṭ pōtarātu aṅṅaṉē niṟka eṉpāḷ niṉmē eṉṟāḷ. kāṟṟāl alippuṇṭamiyāl āmpal  tāmarikku iṟiñcum ūraṉ eṉṟataṉāl, pulaviyāl alippuṇṭavaḻi makaḷirkkut tāḻntu vaṇaṅkuvaṉākaliṉ piṟa makaḷirpāṟ ceṉṟāṉ eṉa uḷḷuṟuttami kāṇka. kōmakaḷ ciṉanteṉa maṭattaki āyam kitoḻutāṅku eṉa niṉṟa veḷippaṭi yuvamam uḷḷuṟiyuvamattic ciṟappittamiyiṉ, tiṇip poruḷ kōṭaṟkut taḷḷātāyiṟṟu.  
viḷakkam : 
“iravuk kuṟiyē illakat tuḷḷum”1 eṉṟa nūṟpā vuriyil ippāṭṭiṉ “aṭṭil ōli toṭṭaṉi niṉmē” eṉṟa aṭiyik kāṭṭi, itu “maṉiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyatu” eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
